\DLine{AB01-PDF0684-body-L031}{}{epicycli occupante), II terminum 59\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} — 5\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 10 = 53\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 50′ (i. e. Luna apogeum excentrici at pe-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0684-body-L032}{}{rigeum epicycli occupante), III terminum 38\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 43′ + 5\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 10′ = 43\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 53′ (Luna in perigeo excen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0684-body-L033}{}{trici at apogeo epicycli exstante), IV terminum 38\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 43′ — 5\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 10′ = 33\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 33′ (Luna in perigeo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0684-body-L034}{}{simul excentrici et epicycli collocata). His quatuor distantiis a Terra parallaxes (pro 90° di-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0684-body-L035}{35}{stantiae verae zenithalis) erunt (4):}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0684-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0684-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) Nota « Jemen C nr. 86 » insignitus; praeter}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0684-notes_left-L002}{}{fragmenta varia complectitur \Name{Hermetis} librum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0684-notes_left-L003}{}{\textit{latitudinis clavis stellarum,} librum sapienti Persici}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0684-notes_left-L004}{}{\textit{Ǵāmāsp} (? cod. \ArabicRun{حاماس}) Arabice a \Name{Muḥammad}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0684-notes_left-L005}{}{\Name{ibn Abī Bakr} al-Fārisī conscripto, Commenta-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0684-notes_left-L006}{}{rium Aḥmad ibn Yūsuf \Name{Ibn ad-Dāyah} in \textit{Cen-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0684-notes_left-L007}{}{\textit{tiloquium} Pseudoptolemaei.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0684-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0684-notes_right-L001}{}{\Indent (2) In edit. Halma perperam minuta, secundae,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0684-notes_right-L002}{}{tertiae pro gradibus, minutis, secundis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0684-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0684-notes_right-L003}{}{\Indent (3) \textit{Almag.} V, 17 (ed. Halma, t. I, p. 348–57);}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0684-notes_right-L004}{}{usus tabularum explicatur V, 19 (Halma I, p. 360-61).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0684-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0684-notes_right-L005}{}{\Indent (4) In numeris apud \Name{Delambre}, \textit{Astron. an-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0684-notes_right-L006}{}{\textit{cienne,} II, p. 218, mendae typographicae non desunt.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0685}{236}
\par\vspace{2.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L001}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{20.90mm}\smash{\rlap{I.}}}\rlap{\hspace*{27.50mm}\smash{\rlap{sin parall. $=$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{54.80mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$\dfrac{1}{64^{p}\,10'}$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{70.30mm}\smash{\llap{$=$ sin}}}\rlap{\hspace*{78.70mm}\smash{\llap{53′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{84.60mm}\smash{\llap{34″}\rlap{.7;}}}\rlap{\hspace*{96.70mm}\smash{\rlap{Ptolemaeus}}}\rlap{\hspace*{124.40mm}\smash{\llap{53′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{130.50mm}\smash{\llap{34″}}}}
\par\vspace{4.65mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L002}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{20.90mm}\smash{\rlap{II.}}}\rlap{\hspace*{27.50mm}\smash{\rlap{sin parall. $=$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{54.80mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$\dfrac{1}{53^{p}\,50'}$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{70.30mm}\smash{\llap{$=$ sin}}}\rlap{\hspace*{78.70mm}\smash{\llap{63′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{84.60mm}\smash{\llap{51″}\rlap{.8;}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.50mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{»}}}\rlap{\hspace*{124.40mm}\smash{\llap{63′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{130.50mm}\smash{\llap{51″}}}}
\par\vspace{4.65mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L003}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{20.90mm}\smash{\rlap{III.}}}\rlap{\hspace*{27.50mm}\smash{\rlap{sin parall. $=$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{54.80mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$\dfrac{1}{43^{p}\,53'}$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{70.30mm}\smash{\llap{$=$ sin}}}\rlap{\hspace*{78.70mm}\smash{\llap{78′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{84.60mm}\smash{\llap{20″}\rlap{.7;}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.50mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{»}}}\rlap{\hspace*{124.40mm}\smash{\llap{79′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{130.50mm}\smash{\llap{0″}}}}
\par\vspace{4.65mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L004}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{20.90mm}\smash{\rlap{IV.}}}\rlap{\hspace*{27.50mm}\smash{\rlap{sin parall. $=$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{54.80mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$\dfrac{1}{33^{p}\,33'}$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{70.30mm}\smash{\llap{$=$ sin}}}\rlap{\hspace*{78.70mm}\smash{\llap{102′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{84.60mm}\smash{\llap{28″}\rlap{.9;}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.50mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{»}}}\rlap{\hspace*{124.40mm}\smash{\llap{104′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{130.50mm}\smash{\llap{0″}\rlap{.}}}}
\par\vspace{3.95mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0685-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L005}{5}{\Indent Ex his parallaxibus horizontalibus $\pi$, pro quoque termino parallaxes altitudinis respon-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L006}{}{dentes distantiae zenithalis verae \textit{z} deducuntur formula antea in Sole memorata.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0685-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L007}{}{\Indent Tabula praebet \textit{parallaxes} primi termini (col. III) ac tertii (col. V), nec non \textit{differentiam}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L008}{}{inter secundum et primum (col. IV), inter quartum et tertium (col. VI), pro quoque secundo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L009}{}{gradu distantiae verae zenithalis Lunae.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0685-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L010}{10}{\Indent Tres reliquae columnae ad supputandas parallaxes in distantiis intermediis a Terra in-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L011}{}{serviunt; idque per partes sexagesimas proportionales. — Ponit \Name{Ptolemaeus} variationes pa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L012}{}{rallaxium proxime esse proportionales ad variationes distantiae a Terra; has autem distantias}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L013}{}{considerat variare ob Lunae motum anomalisticum in circumferentia epicycli et ob motum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L014}{}{centri epicycli in circumferentia excentrici. Centro epicycli occupante apogeum A excentrici,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L015}{15}{anomalia vera seu Lunae elongatio ab apogeo vero H epicycli sit HG = 60°; tunc (1), formula}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L016}{}{qua distantia Lunae a Terra in syzygiis supputatur (cfr. t. I, p. 253–54), invenitur EG esse}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L017}{}{62\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 48′ partium quarum EH (primus terminus) 65\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 15′, EK (secundus terminus) 54\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 45′,}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.278\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0685-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0685-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.702\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.298\FullSourceWidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L018}{}{KH (differentia inter secundum et primum terminum) 10\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 30′}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L019}{}{sunt. Ergo distantia EG a primo termino quantitate — 2\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 27′}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L020}{20}{differt; et si differentia tota KH tamquam unitas (1\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} = 60′)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L021}{}{sumatur, illa 2\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 27′ fient 60′ $\dfrac{2^{p}\,27'}{10^{p}\,30'}=$ 14′. Hinc elucet, in}
\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{2.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L022}{}{distantia apogea centri epicycli et Luna anomaliam veram 60° habente, addendas esse primo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L023}{}{termino (distantiae zenithalis datae respondenti) 14 sexagesimas differentiae inter primum et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L024}{}{secundum terminum eiusdem distantiae zenithalis. — Modo simili operandum erit cum centrum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L025}{25}{epicycli teneat perigeum excentrici (2), id est cum Lunae distantia a Terra sit inter tertium}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L026}{}{et quartum terminum. — Haec omnia, pro quoque secundo gradu \textit{anomaliae verae} supputata,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L027}{}{\Name{Ptolemaeus} in VII col. (centro epicycli apogeum excentrici occupante) et VIII (centro epi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L028}{}{cycli in perigeo excentrici collocato) scripsit. Sed argumentum ambarum columnarum est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L029}{}{\textit{dimidia pars} anomaliae verae, non anomalia integra; qua re sub 30°, non sub 60°, illa 14′ re-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L030}{30}{perientur. Ita \Name{Ptolemaeus} fecit quia etiam argumentum columnae IX dimidiaverat, ut in ta-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L031}{}{bulam non cum dupla elongatione sed cum simplici ingredi opus esset.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0685-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L032}{}{\Indent Hae correctiones sufficiunt cum centrum epicycli teneat apogeum aut perigeum excentrici.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L033}{}{In reliquis punctis nova correctio afferenda, ob diversam distantiam centri epicycli a Terra.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L034}{}{Dupla elongatio media Lunae a Sole sit 60 graduum, hoc est centrum epicycli sumatur ab-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L035}{35}{solvisse 30° excentrici ab apogeo excentrici; ex formula qua distantiae geocentricae extra}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L036}{}{syzygias supputantur (t. I, p. 255–56) colligitur tunc distantiam Lunae a centro Terrae esse}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-body-L037}{}{54\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 3′ partium quarum distantia apogea est 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}, perigea 39\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 22′, earumque differentia 20\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 38′.}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0685-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) In sequentibus calculis omnia referuntur ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-notes_left-L002}{}{distantias Lunae a \textit{centro} (non, ut antea, a superficie)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-notes_left-L003}{}{Terrae; cfr. t. I, p. 77.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0685-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-notes_right-L001}{}{\Indent (2) Tunc in formula ad computandam distan-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0685-notes_right-L002}{}{tiam (t. I, p. 254) pro 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} ponetur 39\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 22′.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0686}{237}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L001}{}{Apogea igitur distantiam datam excedet 5\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 57′, quae, si differentia tota 20\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 38′ tanquam unitas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L002}{}{(1\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} = 60′) sumatur, fient 60′ $\dfrac{5^{p}\,57'}{20^{p}\,38'}=$ 17′ 18″. Haec in IX columna sub 30° scripta legun-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L003}{}{tur, et ad corrigendam parallaxim per anteriores columnas repertam inserviunt. Argumentum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L004}{}{IX columnae est igitur \textit{simplex elongatio media} Lunae a Sole.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0686-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L005}{5}{\Indent Hinc tabularum usus liquet. Cum distantia zenithali vera Lunae in tabulam intra, et sume}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L006}{}{parallaxim primi atque tertii termini (col. III ac V) cum differentiis contiguis (col. IV et VI).}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L007}{}{Anomaliam aequatam Lunae cognosce, aut, si 180° excedit, eius supplementum ad 360°; cum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L008}{}{dimidia eius parte in tabulam introitu facto, sume sexagesimas VII et VIII columnae. Diffe-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L009}{}{rentiam ex IV sumptam multiplica in sexagesimas VII, et differentiam VI columnae in sexage-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L010}{10}{simas VIII; has partes proportionales adde parallaxibus III et V columnae. Exibunt duae pa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L011}{}{rallaxes quarum differentiam supputa. Postea mediam elongationem Lunae a Sole aut a puncto}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L012}{}{diametraliter opposito cognosce, et cum ea in tabulam intrans sexagesimas IX columnae sume,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L013}{}{in quas differentiam parallaxium antea compertam multiplica; productum adde minori duarum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L014}{}{parallaxium; summa erit parallaxis quaesita.}
\par\vspace{4.1mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0686-H15}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L015}{15}{\CenterLine{\textit{Ad pag. 95–101.} — \textbf{Parallaxes lunares longitudinis et latitudinis.}}}
\par\vspace{3.8mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0686-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L016}{}{\Indent Tabulae cap. XL et XLIV (t. I, p. 84, 105, 110) memoratae; earum usus cap. XXXIX}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L017}{}{(t. I, p. 82–84) docetur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0686-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L018}{}{\Indent Ut auctor ipse t. I, p. 82, monet, ex \Name{Theonis} \textit{Tabulis Manualibus} (ed. Halma t. II,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L019}{}{p. 98–111) sumptae, nec ad exactiores supputationes semper bene inservientes; cfr. etiam adnot.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L020}{20}{t. I, p. 262 et addenda eiusdem tomi, p. \textsc{lxxv}. In titulis latitudines climatum \Name{Theonis} (pro obli-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L021}{}{quitate eclipticae 23° 51′ 20″ supputatas) mutavit, sicut nova obliquitas 23° 35′ exposcebat.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0686-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L022}{}{\Indent Locis quibus in versione \textit{culm}[\textit{inationem}] scripsi nihil est in codice praeter lineam rectam,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L023}{}{totam paginam pertranseuntem; ad alteram eius extremitatem \ArabicRun{خط الزوال} « linea meridiei »,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L024}{}{ad alteram \ArabicRun{خط نصف النهار} « linea meridiani » adnotatum est. Malui \textit{culminationem} vertere,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L025}{25}{\Name{Schiaparelli} suadente, nam iis locis de transitu primi puncti Cancri, primi Leonis etc. per}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L026}{}{meridianum agitur. — Ut melius quam e solis titulis \textit{austr.} et \textit{bor.} appareret quales paral-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L027}{}{laxes latitudinis negativae, quales positivae essent, positivis signum + praeposui. Parallaxes}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L028}{}{longitudinis quando sint additivae, quando subtractivae elucet e praeceptis t. I, p. 84 et 262–63 (1).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0686-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L029}{}{\Indent Numeros codicis mendosos facile erat sanare, nam in singulis climatibus semper bis re-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L030}{30}{petuntur eaedem parallaxes; collatio cum \Name{Theone} certiores etiam emendationes faciebat.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L031}{}{Tabulae Theonianae non paucis typothetae lapsibus in editione Halma scatent, pluribus in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L032}{}{eiusdem versione Gallica.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0686-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L033}{}{\Indent In titulis tabularum numeros latitudinum climatum servaveram sicut in codice inveneram;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L034}{}{sed iniuria, nam haec emendanda sunt:}
\par\vspace{2.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L035}{35}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{17.10mm}\smash{\rlap{Pag.}}}\rlap{\hspace*{32.90mm}\smash{\llap{95:}}}\rlap{\hspace*{41.70mm}\smash{\llap{16°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{47.40mm}\smash{\llap{39′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{51.00mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{\ArabicRun{لط}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{54.90mm}\smash{\rlap{pro 16° 32′ \ArabicRun{لب} cod. (praecise 16° 38′ 48″)}}}}
\par\vspace{0.6mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L036}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{20.40mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{»}}}\rlap{\hspace*{32.90mm}\smash{\llap{96:}}}\rlap{\hspace*{41.70mm}\smash{\llap{24°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{47.40mm}\smash{\llap{5′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{51.00mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{\ArabicRun{ه}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{54.90mm}\smash{\rlap{pro 24° cod. (omisso \AbjadZero{} 0′; praecise 24° 4′ 47″)}}}}
\par\vspace{1.2mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L037}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{20.40mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{»}}}\rlap{\hspace*{32.90mm}\smash{\llap{101:}}}\rlap{\hspace*{41.70mm}\smash{\llap{48°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{47.40mm}\smash{\llap{53′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{51.00mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{\ArabicRun{نج}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{54.90mm}\smash{\rlap{pro 48° 13′ \ArabicRun{يج} cod. (praecise 48° 52′ 46″).}}}}
\par\vspace{1.6mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L038}{}{Duae priores mendae etiam in tabulis p. 65 occurrebant in codice, quas in versione correxi.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-body-L039}{}{Pag. 97, ad II horam Cancri post culminationem, parall. long. 22′ restituatur pro 21′.}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0686-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) Parallaxes longitudinis, \textit{hora culminationis}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-notes_left-L002}{}{dati signi, positivae sunt a Cancro ad Capricornum,}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-notes_right-L001}{}{negativae a Capricorno ad Cancrum, nullae in Cancro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0686-notes_right-L002}{}{et Capricorno.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0687}{238}
\par\vspace{1mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0687-H01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L001}{}{\CenterLine{\textit{Ad pag. 102-106.} — \textbf{Motus medii planetarum ad calendarium Romanum.}}}
\par\vspace{2.8mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0687-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L002}{}{\Indent Annus Romanus hic est annus \textit{fictus} a 1 ādhār (Martii) anni chronologici iam incepti}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L003}{}{computatus, de quo antea p. 227 dixi. Cur medius anomaliae motus in planetis superioribus,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L004}{}{medius longitudinis in inferioribus omissus sit, iam p. 204 exposui. — Motus omnes harum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L005}{5}{tabularum aliquantulo a \Name{Ptolemaicis} (1) discrepant longis temporis intervallis; cfr. t. I,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L006}{}{p. 239 et 242.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0687-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L007}{}{\Indent De titulis columnarum p. 102, eadem repetenda quae p. 203–04 memoravi. — P. 103, minuta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L008}{}{motus anomaliae Mercurii, annis singulis a 10 ad 17, rudiori modo supputata, 1′ et 2′ a iustis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L009}{}{discrepant. — P. 104, ad mensem subāṭ librarius ubique 0 scripserat; numeros restitui, et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L010}{10}{subāṭ bisextilium (quamquam supervacaneus est, cfr. t. II, p. 223, ad II, 74) addidi. — P. 105,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L011}{}{horarum, et 106, dierum, necessario cum p. 28 et 27 congruunt.}
\par\vspace{3.3mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0687-H12}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L012}{}{\CenterLine{\textit{Ad pag. 108-137.} — \textbf{Aequationes planetarum.}}}
\par\vspace{2mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0687-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L013}{}{\Indent Tabulae memoratae XXXI et XLV cap. (t. I, p. 64 et 113-14).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0687-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L014}{}{\Indent Innituntur \Name{Ptolemaicis} XI, 11 (ed. Halma t. II, p. 300–09), quibus titulus: \textgreek{ἔκϑεσις}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L015}{15}{\textgreek{κανόνων τῆς κατὰ μῆκος τῶν ε}′ \textgreek{πλανωμένων διευκρινήσεως} « expositio tabularum correctionis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L016}{}{« quinque planetarum in longitudine ». \Name{Ptolemaeus} argumentum per senos gradus in primo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L017}{}{ultimoque quadrante disposuerat, per ternos in secundo ac tertio; \Name{al-Battānī} per singulos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L018}{}{gradus semper (2), quantitates Ptolemaicas recipiens, reliquas interpolans. Columnarum titulos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L019}{}{aptius quam Ptolemaeus delegit. Peropportune demum in unam fudit duas columnas Ptolemaei}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L020}{20}{quae \textgreek{μήκους προσϑαφαίρεσις} « prosthaphaeresis (3) longitudinis » et \textgreek{διάφορα προσϑέσεως} (aut \textgreek{δ}. \textgreek{ἀφαι}-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L021}{}{\textgreek{ρέσεως}) « differentiae additionis » (aut « diff. subtractionis ») inscriptae sunt; ergo III columna}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L022}{}{Albatenii (aequatio anomaliae et centri) est summa \textit{algebrica} columnarum III et IV Ptolemaei,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L023}{}{ut infra ostendam. — Albatenii IV est Ptolemaei VIII; reliquae, id est V, VI et VII eundem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L024}{}{numerum apud utrumque habent.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0687-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L025}{25}{\Indent \Name{Ḥabash} f. 42,r.–44,v., 46,r.–48,v., 50,r.–52,v., 54,r.–56,v., 58,r.–60,v., tabulas aequa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L026}{}{tionum eodem quo \Name{al-Battānī} modo conditas praebet.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0687-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L027}{}{\Indent Duo codicis lapsus etiam emendandi: in III columna p. 116, ult. l., et p. 117, l. 1\textsuperscript{a},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L028}{}{5° 15′ \ArabicRun{يه} legatur pro 5° 14′ \ArabicRun{يد} (4).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0687-P07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L029}{}{\Indent Motus longitudinis \textit{planetarum superiorum} et \textit{Veneris} ita in systemate \Name{Ptolemaei}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L030}{30}{explicantur. Unicuique horum quatuor planetarum circulus \textit{excentricus} (\textgreek{ὁ ἔκκεντρος}) respectu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L031}{}{centri Terrae vel eclipticae est, in cuius peripheria centrum \textit{epicycli} movetur; plerumque ab}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L032}{}{Arabibus eorumque asseclis e nostratibus \textit{deferens} (\textit{al-ḥāmil}) vocatur (5). Centrum epicycli}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L033}{}{secundum successionem signorum (ab occasu ad ortum) circumferentiam deferentis ambit, sed}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L034}{}{motu inaequali tam respectu centri M deferentis quam centri E Terrae; motu aequabili autem circa}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L035}{35}{punctum E′, a centro M deferentis tanto distans quanto in opposita parte centrum E Terrae. Fictus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L036}{}{hic circulus, eiusdem diametri cuius deferens, a \Name{Ptolemaeo} \textgreek{ἔκκεντρος τῆς ὁμαλῆς κινήσεως}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L037}{}{(seu \textgreek{παρόδου}) « excentricus motus aequabilis », ab Arabibus \textit{al-mu‘addil li ’l-masīr} « aequans}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-body-L038}{}{motum », a nostratibus \textit{aequans} vocatur; merus circulus, non sphaera seu orbis consideratur.}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0687-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) Tabulae \Name{Ptolemaei} praeterea ad aliam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-notes_left-L002}{}{aeram et ad annos vagos (sine intercalatione Iuliana)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-notes_left-L003}{}{referuntur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0687-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-notes_left-L004}{}{\Indent (2) Ita iam \Name{Theon}, ed. Halma, II, p. 134-93.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0687-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-notes_left-L005}{}{\Indent (3) I. e. quantitas modo addenda, modo demenda;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-notes_left-L006}{}{\textit{aequatio} Arabum et nostratum.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0687-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-notes_right-L001}{}{\Indent (4) In \textit{Almagesti} ed. Halma, aequatio anomaliae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-notes_right-L002}{}{Veneris respondens 174 gradibus perperam 14° 41′}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-notes_right-L003}{}{pro 14° 47′ scribitur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0687-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-notes_right-L004}{}{\Indent (5) Apud \Name{Ptolemaeum} interdum \textgreek{ὁ φέρων τὸν}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-notes_right-L005}{}{\textgreek{ἐπίκυκλον ἔκκεντρος} vel \textgreek{ὁ ἔκκεντρος κύκλος ἐφ’ οὗ φέ}-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0687-notes_right-L006}{}{\textgreek{ρεται τὸ τοῦ ἐπικύκλου κέντρον}.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0688}{239}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0688-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L001}{}{\Indent Centrum epicycli \textit{Veneris} ambitum deferentis conficit anno sidereo Solis; \textit{trium supe-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L002}{}{\textit{riorum} aliis temporis intervallis. \textit{Venus} igitur semper est in media cum Sole coniunctione. —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L003}{}{\textit{Superiores} uno anno sidereo circumferentiam epicycli absolvunt (1), et quotannis semel (in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L004}{}{apogeo vero epicycli consistentes) Soli coniunguntur coniunctione media, semel (in perigeo vero}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L005}{5}{epicycli) media oppositione opponuntur. \textit{Venus} autem medio motu epicyclum conficit 583 diebus,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L006}{}{22 horis, 15 minutis, seu 19 mensibus circiter solaribus.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0688-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L007}{}{\Indent Linea KG absidum excentrici constantem directionem servat respectu stellarum fixarum;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L008}{}{in \textit{Venere} et \textit{Marte} coincidit cum linea maximae inclinationis plani excentrici ad planum}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{99.00mm}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L009}{}{eclipticae; in \textit{Iove} 20° ab occasu huius lineae deflectit; in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L010}{10}{\textit{Saturno} 50° ab ortu. Apogea excentricorum \textit{Saturni, Iovis,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L011}{}{\textit{Martis} semper in boream ab ecliptica vergunt, perigea semper}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L012}{}{in austrum; at apogeum et perigeum \textit{Veneris} omnes vices}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L013}{}{subeunt, modo borealem, modo australem latitudinem sor-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L014}{}{tientia, nonnunquam vero in eclipticae plano consistentia. —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L015}{15}{Eadem linea KG circa centrum E Terrae motu praecessionis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L016}{}{seu fixarum movetur, secum puncta E′ ac M ferens.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0688-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L017}{}{\Indent In quatuor his planetis excentricitas est EM, cuius du-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L018}{}{plum EE′. Apogeum epicycli \textit{verum} est quodcumque in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L019}{}{exteriore semicircumferentia epicycli punctum in quod cadit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L020}{20}{linea recta ex centro E Terrae per centrum H epicycli pro-}
\end{minipage}\hspace{2mm}\begin{minipage}[t]{87.00mm}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0688-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0688-F01}\end{minipage}\par
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L021}{}{ducta; apogeum epicycli \textit{medium} punctum in quod cadit linea recta ex aequantis centro E′}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L022}{}{per centrum epicycli ducta. Medium et verum concurrunt quotiescumque centrum epicycli}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L023}{}{est in apogeo G aut perigeo K excentrici.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0688-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L024}{}{\Indent In tribus superioribus linea ex centro epicycli ad planetam ducta, in coniunctionibus et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L025}{25}{oppositionibus mediis cum Sole coincidit cum linea medii motus Solis; extra coniunctiones et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L026}{}{oppositiones illi est parallela. In inferioribus linea medii motus in excentrico est parallela lineae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L027}{}{medii motus Solis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0688-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L028}{}{\Indent Circuli antea memorati in diversis ab eclipticae planis iacent; sed quoniam eorum incli-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L029}{}{nationes sensibiliter non mutant longitudines planetarum, ad explicandos motus longitudinis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L030}{30}{\Name{Ptolemaeus} circulos omnes in eodem plano quo ecliptica descriptos esse ponit (2), sicut et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L031}{}{in Luna fecerat.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0688-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L032}{}{\Indent Sit ABD ecliptica, GHK deferens, H centrum epicycli dato temporis momento, G apogeum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L033}{}{excentrici (cuius longitudo in ecliptica A). Iuxta suum motum medium circa centrum E′ ae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L034}{}{quantis, centrum epicycli in ecliptica longitudinem Z tenebit; sed iuxta motum verum, sicut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L035}{35}{e centro E Terrae cernitur, longitudinem X numerabit. Arcus ZX, differentia inter locum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-body-L036}{}{medium et verum, a \Name{Ptolemaeo} \textgreek{ἡ ζωδιακὴ ἀνωμαλία} « inaequalitas zodiacalis », ab \Name{Alba-}}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0688-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) Planetae omnes in superiore medietate epi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-notes_left-L002}{}{cycli ad successionem signorum (ab occasu ad or-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-notes_left-L003}{}{tum) moventur, contra in inferiore. Trium \textit{superio-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-notes_left-L004}{}{\textit{rum} motus in epicyclo adaequat excessum medii}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-notes_left-L005}{}{motus longitudinis Solis super ipsorum medios mo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-notes_left-L006}{}{tus centri epicycli.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0688-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-notes_left-L007}{}{\Indent (2) \textit{Almag.} IX, 6 (ed. Halma, t. II, p. 160). —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-notes_left-L008}{}{Alibi, supputationem latitudinum planetarum docens}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-notes_left-L009}{}{(XIII, 4, ed. Halma, t. II, p. 386, 388, 393-94, 397, 400),}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-notes_right-L001}{}{demonstrat maximam differentiam additivam aut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-notes_right-L002}{}{subtractivam longitudinibus ob \textit{inclinationem} epi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-notes_right-L003}{}{cycli contingentem esse 2′ in Venere, 3′ in Mercurio,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-notes_right-L004}{}{1′ in Saturno, 1′ in Iove, nullam in Marte (calculis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-notes_right-L005}{}{exactius ductis, \Name{Delambre}, in notis ad Almage-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-notes_right-L006}{}{stum ed. Halma, invenit 1′ 40″, 6′ 55″, 0′ 17″, 0′ 15″,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-notes_right-L007}{}{1′ 31″); maximam autem ex \textit{reflexione} epicycli exo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-notes_right-L008}{}{rientem 1′ in Venere, 6′ in Mercurio (Halma, II,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0688-notes_right-L009}{}{p. 406, 407; \Name{Delambre} in notis 1′ 0″, 7′ 29″).}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0689}{240}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-body-L001}{}{\Name{tenio} Arabibusque « \textit{aequatio anomaliae et centri} » (1) vocatur, unde nomen \textit{aequationis}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-body-L002}{}{\textit{centri} quo hodierni etiam astronomi utuntur. Hanc aequationem metitur angulus XHZ = E′HE.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-body-L003}{}{Ductis perpendicularibus EN et MS ad lineam rectam ZHE′ productam, statim apparet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-body-L004}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{41.40mm}sin aequat. centri $=$ sin $E'HE=R\,\dfrac{NE}{HE}$\,.}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-body-L005}{5}{Sit \textit{m} quod Arabes et nostrates aevi medii \textit{centrum} (\textit{al-markaz}) vocant, sive arcus eclipticae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-body-L006}{}{AZ inter longitudinem apogei excentrici et medium locum planetae interceptus; eum metitur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-body-L007}{}{angulus AE′Z in centro aequantis. Sit \textit{e} excentricitas, seu EM = ME′; tunc}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-body-L008}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{45.90mm}$NE=E'E\,\dfrac{\sin NE'E}{R}=2e\,\dfrac{\sin m}{R}$\,.}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-body-L009}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}Restat ut}\rlap{\hspace*{18.90mm}$HE=\sqrt{\overline{HN}^2+\overline{NE}^2}=\sqrt{(HS+SN)^2+\overline{NE}^2}$ cognoscamus.}}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.276\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0689-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0689-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.704\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.296\FullSourceWidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-body-L010}{10}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}$HS=\sqrt{\overline{HM}^2-\overline{MS}^2}$\,,\quad ubi\quad $HM=$ radius deferentis $=\rho$.}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-body-L011}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{20.10mm}$MS=E'M\,\dfrac{\sin ME'S}{R}=e\,\dfrac{\sin m}{R}$\,,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-body-L012}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}ergo}\rlap{\hspace*{28.40mm}$HS=\sqrt{\rho^2-\left(\dfrac{e\sin m}{R}\right)^2}$\,.}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-body-L013}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}Praeterea}\rlap{\hspace*{19.10mm}$SN=E'N-E'S$}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-body-L014}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{5.50mm}$E'N=E'E\,\dfrac{\sin NEE'}{R}=E'E\,\dfrac{\cos NE'E}{R}=2\,e\,\dfrac{\cos m}{R}$}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-body-L015}{15}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{5.90mm}$E'S=E'M\,\dfrac{\sin E'MS}{R}=E'M\,\dfrac{\cos NE'E}{R}=e\,\dfrac{\cos m}{R}$\,.}}
\end{minipage}\par
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-body-L016}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}Ergo}\rlap{\hspace*{18.60mm}$\overline{HE}^2=\Bigl\{\sqrt{\rho^2-\left(\dfrac{e\sin m}{R}\right)^2}+\dfrac{2e\cos m}{R}-\dfrac{e\cos m}{R}\Bigr\}^2+\left(\dfrac{2e\sin m}{R}\right)^2$}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-body-L017}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{22.60mm}$HE=\sqrt{\Bigl\{\sqrt{\rho^2-\left(\dfrac{e\sin m}{R}\right)^2}+\dfrac{e\cos m}{R}\Bigr\}^2+\left(\dfrac{2e\sin m}{R}\right)^2}$\,,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-body-L018}{}{quae est \textit{distantia geocentrica centri epicycli.} Unde demum:}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-body-L019}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{26.60mm}sin aequat. centri $=\dfrac{2e\sin m}{\text{dist.\ centri epicycli a Terrae centro}}$\,.}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-body-L020}{20}{Qua formula, constructionibus geometricis \Name{Ptolemaei} deducta, procul dubio \Name{al-Battānī}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-body-L021}{}{aliique multi (ac ipse \Name{Regiomontanus}) tabulam aequationis centri condiderunt aut pro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-body-L022}{}{baverunt.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0689-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-body-L023}{}{\Indent Tangentibus adhibitis, regula longe simplicior reperitur:}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-body-L024}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{9.70mm}tang aequat. centri $=$ tang $E'HE=R\,\dfrac{NE}{HN}=\dfrac{2e\sin m}{\sqrt{\rho^2-\left(\dfrac{e\sin m}{R}\right)^2}+\dfrac{e\cos m}{R}}$\,;}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-body-L025}{25}{\textit{R} autem ac \textgreek{ρ} unius partis sumptis, ac \textit{e} ad \textgreek{ρ} = 1 relata:}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-body-L026}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{31.60mm}tang aequat. centri $=\dfrac{2e_1\sin m}{\sqrt{1-(e_1\sin m)^2}+e_1\cos m}$\,.}}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0689-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) \textit{Ta‘dīl al-ḥāṣṣah wa ’l-markaz;} eius quan-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-notes_left-L002}{}{titas enim est etiam differentia inter epicycli apo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-notes_left-L003}{}{geum verum et medium, qua uti oportet ad ano-}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-notes_right-L001}{}{maliam mediam in veram convertendam. Quid \textit{cen-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0689-notes_right-L002}{}{\textit{trum} sit infra statim dicam.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0690}{241}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0690-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L001}{}{\Indent Hac aequatione tam discrepantia cognoscitur ex inaequali centri epicycli motu exoriens,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L002}{}{quam differentia inter \textit{anomaliam mediam} (seu arcum epicycli ab apogeo epicycli medio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L003}{}{computatum) et \textit{veram} \textgreek{ψ} (arcum epicycli ab apogeo vero epicycli numeratum). — Talis aequatio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L004}{}{in III columna \Name{Albatenii} exhibetur. — \Name{Ptolemaeus} vero hanc inaequalitatem in ecliptica}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L005}{5}{exorientem ex centri epicycli motu in deferente (quam « \textit{zodiacalem inaequalitatem} », \textgreek{ἡ ζω}-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L006}{}{\textgreek{διακὴ ἀνωμαλία}, vocat) non solum in constructionibus geometricis, sed etiam in tabulis semper}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L007}{}{in duas partes distinguit; quarum altera (\textgreek{προσϑαφαίρεσις μήκους} « prosthaphaeresis longitudinis »)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L008}{}{est aequatio ita simpliciter (\textgreek{κατὰ τὸ ἁπλοῦν}) supputata ac si centrum epicycli circumferentiam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L009}{}{aequantis perambularet, non deferentis; qua re, $2e_1$ vocata excentricitate aequantis in par-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L010}{10}{tibus decimalibus radii aequantis, radioque ipso aequantis unius partis sumpto, III columnam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L011}{}{\Name{Ptolemaei} nos supputare possumus formula}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L012}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{34.10mm}tang prosthaphaeresis longitudinis $=\dfrac{2e_1\sin m}{1+2e_1\cos m}$\,,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L013}{}{sicut in hypothesi excentrici Lunae operandum diximus. Sed quoniam centrum epicycli in cir-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L014}{}{cumferentia deferentis, non aequantis, veraciter movetur, distantia centri epicycli a centro ae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L015}{15}{quantis bis tantum in tota circumferentia deferentis erit 1 (aut 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} iuxta veterum usum);}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L016}{}{pro reliquis omnibus locis igitur prosthaphaeresis longitudinis corrigi debebit. Quaerit ergo \Name{Pto-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L017}{}{\Name{lemaeus} quanto crescat in perigeo, quanto decrescat in apogeo; has correctiones prostha-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L018}{}{phaeresibus longitudinis afferendas in IV columna suarum tabularum (\textgreek{διάφορα προσϑέσεως} aut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L019}{}{\textgreek{διάφορα ἀφαιρέσεως}) constituit. Res e sequenti diagrammate clarior apparebit. Prosthaphaeresim}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L020}{20}{longitudinis, seu III columnam, \Name{Ptolemaeus} supputat ac si centrum epicycli in puncto \textit{h}}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{119.50mm}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L021}{}{aequantis esset, non in puncto H deferentis; loco igitur verae anomaliae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L022}{}{zodiacalis (seu aequationis centri) EHE′ considerat angulum EhE′ (pro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L023}{}{sthaphaeresim longitudinis). Error inde proveniens est angulus hEH, modo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L024}{}{prosthaphaeresi addendus, modo demendus; in IV columna \Name{Ptolemaei}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L025}{25}{exhibetur. — Summa algebrica columnarum III et IV aequationem centri}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L026}{}{conficit. Sed \Name{Ptolemaeus} ipse XI, 10 (ed. Halma t. II, p. 295) haec}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L027}{}{monet: « Hic vero, utpote in Syntaxi, oportebat talem distinctionem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L028}{}{« inaequalitatis zodiacalis ante oculos facere, ideoque in duabus co-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L029}{}{« lumnis collocare; sed in eius usu etiam una columna, ex summa al-}
\end{minipage}\hspace{2mm}\begin{minipage}[t]{66.50mm}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0690-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0690-F01}\end{minipage}\par
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L030}{30}{« gebrica illarum duarum collecta, sufficiet » (\textgreek{ἐνϑάδε μὲν οὖν, ὡς ἐν συντάξει, προσῆκον ἦν τὴν}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L031}{}{\textgreek{τοιαύτην διάκρισιν τῆς ζωδιακῆς ἀνωμαλίας ὑπ’ ὄψιν ποιήσασϑαι, καὶ διὰ τοῦτο ἐν δυσὶ σελιδίοις ἐκϑέσϑαι},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L032}{}{\textgreek{ἐπὶ μέντοι τῆς χρείας αὐτῆς ἀπαρκέσει καὶ ἓν σελίδιον ἐκ τῆς ἀμφοτέρων τούτων προσϑαφαιρέσεως}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L033}{}{\textgreek{ἐπισυνηγμένον}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0690-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L034}{}{\Indent Alteram correctionem nunc longitudini inferre oportet ob varium planetae situm in cir-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L035}{35}{cumferentia \textit{epicycli.}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0690-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L036}{}{\Indent Sit planeta in T; quaeritur arcus XY seu angulus XEY. Perpendiculari Tt ducta:}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L037}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{37.20mm}sin $XEY=R\,\dfrac{Tt}{ET}=R\,\dfrac{Tt}{\text{dist.\ geocentr.\ planetae}}$}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L038}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{48.40mm}$Tt=\dfrac{TH\times\sin XHT}{R}=\dfrac{r\sin\psi}{R}$\,,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L039}{}{ubi \textit{r} est radius epicycli notus, in partibus qualium 60 radium deferentis et aequantis conficiunt.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0690-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L040}{40}{\Indent Distantia autem planetae a centro Terrae est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0690-body-L041}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{51.40mm}$ET=\sqrt{(EH+Ht)^2+Tt^2}$\,;}}
\par\vspace{5mm}\hfill{\fontsize{8}{10}\selectfont 31}\hspace{10mm}
\DSource{0691}{242}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L001}{}{sed EH = distantia geocentrica centri epicycli, antea reperta,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L002}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{48.60mm}$Ht=\dfrac{TH\times\sin HTt}{R}=\dfrac{r\cos\psi}{R}$\,,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L003}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}ergo}\rlap{\hspace*{27.70mm}$ET=\sqrt{\left(\text{dist.\ centri epicycli}+\dfrac{r\cos\psi}{R}\right)^2+\left(\dfrac{r\sin\psi}{R}\right)^2}$\,.}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L004}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}Demum}\rlap{\hspace*{30.70mm}sin aequat. anomaliae $=\dfrac{r\sin\psi}{\text{distantia geocentrica planetae}}$\,.}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0691-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L005}{5}{\Indent Haec \textit{aequatio anomaliae} (\textgreek{ἀνωμαλίας προσϑαφαίρεσις}) est differentia inter verum locum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L006}{}{centri epicycli et verum locum planetae in ecliptica, seu est arcus eclipticae contentus linea}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.322\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0691-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0691-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.658\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.342\FullSourceWidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L007}{}{veri loci centri epicycli et linea veri loci planetae. —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L008}{}{Tangentibus adhibitis, formula fit:}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L009}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{6.20mm}tang aequat. anomaliae $=$ tang $XEY=R\,\dfrac{Tt}{Et}$}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L010}{10}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{5.70mm}$Et=EH+Ht=$ dist. centri epicycli $+\,\dfrac{r\cos\psi}{R}$}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L011}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}tang aequat. anomaliae $=\dfrac{r\sin\psi}{\text{dist.\ centri epic.}+\dfrac{r\cos\psi}{R}}$\,,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L012}{}{vel, R = 1 facto ut etiam radio deferentis, et \textit{r} in par-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L013}{}{tibus decimalibus radii deferentis sumpto:}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L014}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}tang aequat. anomaliae $=\dfrac{r_1\sin\psi}{\text{dist.\ centri epic.}+r_1\cos\psi}$\,.}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0691-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L015}{15}{\Indent E constructionibus factis colligitur:}
\end{minipage}\par
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0691-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L016}{}{\Indent 1.° Aequationem centri nullam fieri, centro epicycli apogeum aut perigeum excentrici}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L017}{}{(deferentis) occupante; maximam in binis punctis mediis. Locis deferentis diametraliter oppositis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L018}{}{aequalem esse, sicut et locis utrimque ab apogeo aut a perigeo deferentis aeque distantibus.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0691-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L019}{}{\Indent 2.° Aequationem centri esse centro medio subducendam in priore semicirculo AYB}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L020}{20}{(in quo longitudo X veri centri praecedit longitudinem Z centri medii), addendam in posteriore. —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L021}{}{Contrario modo eandem aequationem centri esse anomaliae mediae addendam et subducendam.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0691-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L022}{}{\Indent 3.° Aequationem anomaliae nullam esse cum, centro epicycli apogeum aut perigeum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L023}{}{excentrici (deferentis) obtinente, planeta apogeum aut perigeum epicycli occupet; tunc enim}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L024}{}{epicycli apogeum et perigeum vera cum mediis coincidunt. Maximam quando centrum epicycli}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L025}{25}{in perigeo excentrici sit, planeta autem in eo epicycli puncto quod designatur linea tangente}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L026}{}{ex centro mundi ad circumferentiam epicycli ducta.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0691-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L027}{}{\Indent 4.° Aequationem anomaliae in priore semicirculo epicycli addendam esse longitudini veri}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L028}{}{centri, in posteriore demendam.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0691-P07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L029}{}{\Indent Una columna sufficeret ad aequationem anomaliae exhibendam si centrum epicycli eandem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L030}{30}{semper distantiam a centro Terrae servaret. Quod cum non fiat, ad aequandos motus absque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L031}{}{nimio tabularum numero oportebit variationes aequationibus contingentibus ob diversas distantias}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L032}{}{indicare per minuta proportionalia. Quoniam variatio a perigeo ad apogeum in his quinque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L033}{}{planetis adeo est magna, ut minuta proportionalia, si simpliciter constituantur sicut in Luna}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L034}{}{factum est, errores sensibiles gignant, \Name{Ptolemaeus}, quem \Name{al-Battānī} sequitur, in VI co-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L035}{35}{lumna aequationem anomaliae pro media distantia centri epicycli a Terra posuit; in V quan-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L036}{}{titatem a sexta demendam ut aequatio pro maxima epicycli distantia exeat; in VII excessum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0691-body-L037}{}{aequationis pro minima distantia super aequationem pro media; in VIII demum minuta pro-}
\DSource{0692}{243}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L001}{}{portionalia ad aequationes eruendas in distantiis intermediis. Haec minuta proportionalia sup-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L002}{}{putata sunt pro maximis aequationibus, nempe iis quae contingunt in tangente a centro Terrae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L003}{}{ad circumferentiam epicycli ducta; correctiones enim aequationum ceteris epicycli punctis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L004}{}{contingentium eandem proportionem sensibiliter servant (cfr. \textit{Almag.} XI, 10, ed. Halma t. II,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L005}{5}{p. 296–99). — Ergo, iis comparatis quae de tabulis lunaribus diximus (p. 225), praecepta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L006}{}{\Name{Albatenii} t. I, p. 113–114, facile intelligentur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0692-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L007}{}{\Indent Perplexior res est in \textit{Mercurio;} in quo centrum M deferentis (1) non obtinet medium locum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L008}{}{inter centrum E′ aequantis et centrum E Terrae, sed circellum (ab Arabibus \textit{al-mudīr} « re-}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{112.00mm}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L009}{}{volventem » vocatum) ambit, cuius centrum F ab M et ab E′ tanto}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L010}{10}{distat quanto centrum aequantis a centro Terrae. Qua re interdum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L011}{}{centrum deferentis in centrum aequantis cadit; tunc punctum apogei}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L012}{}{deferentis perigeum aequantis obtinet, amboque circuli coincidunt (2).}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L013}{}{Linea absidum aequantis circa E ad motum praecessionis movetur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L014}{}{sicut in reliquis planetis. Centrum epicycli circumferentiam deferentis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L015}{15}{secundum successionem signorum uno anno sidereo Solis conficit;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L016}{}{eodemque tempore, contra successionem signorum (ab ortu ad occa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L017}{}{sum) centrum deferentis circellum revolventem perambulat, et linea}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L018}{}{absidum deferentis integrum circulum describit; unde fit ut, ob sum-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L019}{}{mam horum aequalium sed contrariorum motuum, bis in anno}
\end{minipage}\hspace{2mm}\begin{minipage}[t]{74.00mm}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0692-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0692-F01}\end{minipage}\par
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L020}{20}{sidereo centrum epicycli apogeum excentrici (deferentis) teneat, bis perigeum. Eadem causa}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L021}{}{semper tanta est distantia angularis apogei aequantis ab apogeo deferentis quanta, in opposita}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L022}{}{parte, a centro epicycli. — Medius motus centri epicycli medium motum longitudinis Solis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L023}{}{adaequat; Mercurius autem diebus circiter 115, horis 21, minutis 5, epicyclum suum motu}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{95.50mm}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L024}{}{medio absolvit. — Minima epicycli distantia a Terra tum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L025}{25}{contingit cum centrum epicycli ex alterutra parte ab apogeo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L026}{}{aequantis 120° circuli gradibus absit; non vero cum peri-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L027}{}{geum fixum aequantis aut perigeum mobile deferentis te-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L028}{}{neat. Idque ob aequales sed contrarios motus centri epicycli}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L029}{}{et centri deferentis. — Linea absidum aequantis est in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L030}{30}{linea maximae inclinationis plani excentrici ad planum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L031}{}{eclipticae; apogeum et perigeum modo borealia, modo au-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L032}{}{stralia, modo in eclipticae plano consistunt.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0692-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L033}{}{\Indent Nunc Mercurii aequationes supputemus. Sit GK linea}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L034}{}{absidum aequantis; dato temporis momento centrum M}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L035}{35}{deferentis motu suo retrogrado ad punctum I pervenisse}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L036}{}{ponatur. Ob ea quae antea dixi, centrum epicycli tunc in H}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L037}{}{erit, ita ut angulus GE′H (seu centrum medium \textit{m}) aeque-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-body-L038}{}{tur angulo GFI. Erit X longitudo apogei veri P epicycli,}
\end{minipage}\hspace{2mm}\begin{minipage}[t]{90.50mm}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0692-body-F02}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0692-F02}\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0692-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) In \textit{Hypothesibus} ed. Halma p. 48, l. 10, cen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-notes_left-L002}{}{tra aequantis et deferentis Mercurii intra eclipticae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-notes_left-L003}{}{vel Terrae centrum et limitem \textit{australem} (\textgreek{νότιον}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-notes_left-L004}{}{\textgreek{πέρας}) collocantur; sed forte error est illius parum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-notes_left-L005}{}{bonae editionis pro \textit{boreali.}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0692-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-notes_left-L006}{}{\Indent (2) Excentrici (deferentis) radio 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} sumpto,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-notes_left-L007}{}{distantia puncti E ab E′, sicut E′ ab F, in \textit{Alma-}}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-notes_right-L001}{}{\textit{gesto} 3\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} statuitur; qua re excentricitas deferentis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-notes_right-L002}{}{intra 3\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} et 9\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} variat. In serioribus \textit{Hypothesibus,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-notes_right-L003}{}{p. 48, distantia E ab E′ 3\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} servata, radius FE′ cir-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-notes_right-L004}{}{celli 2\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 50′ sumitur, ita ut excentricitas intra 3\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-notes_right-L005}{}{8\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 40′ variet. Hinc fractiones apud \Name{Tannery}, \textit{Re-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-notes_right-L006}{}{\textit{cherches sur l’hist. de l’astr. ancienne,} p. 251,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0692-notes_right-L007}{}{l. 4-8, intelliguntur.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0693}{244}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L001}{}{Z (in linea recta ZHE′) longitudo apogei medii Q epicycli; ergo, planeta in T (cuius longitudo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L002}{}{vera Y) exsistente, QT anomalia media, PT anomalia vera.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0693-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L003}{}{\Indent E tabulis mediorum motuum colliguntur \textit{m} et arcus QT; nota est quoque excentricitas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L004}{}{\textit{e} = EE′ = E′F = FI. — In triangulo isosceli IFE′ est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L005}{5}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{50.30mm}$IE':E'F=\sin IFE':\sin FIE'$}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L006}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}vel}\rlap{\hspace*{43.80mm}$IE':\;e\;=\sin\,(180^\circ-m):\sin\left(\dfrac{m}{2}\right)$\,,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L007}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}unde}\rlap{\hspace*{47.60mm}$IE'=\dfrac{e\sin(180^\circ-m)}{\sin\left(\dfrac{m}{2}\right)}=\dfrac{e\sin m}{\sin\left(\dfrac{m}{2}\right)}$\,;}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L008}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}et quia}\rlap{\hspace*{41.50mm}$\sin m=\sin 2\left(\dfrac{m}{2}\right)=2\sin\left(\dfrac{m}{2}\right)\cos\left(\dfrac{m}{2}\right)$\,,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L009}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}erit demum}\rlap{\hspace*{56.10mm}$IE'=2e\cos\left(\dfrac{m}{2}\right)$\,.}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0693-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L010}{10}{\Indent In triangulo E′IH est angulus\quad $IE'H=m+\dfrac{m}{2}$\,,\quad radius autem deferentis IH = 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}};}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L011}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}ergo}\rlap{\hspace*{29.00mm}$E'H=\dfrac{IE'\times\cos IE'H}{R}+\sqrt{\overline{IH}^2-\left(\dfrac{IE'\times\sin IE'H}{R}\right)^2}$}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L012}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{10.80mm}$E'H=\dfrac{2e}{R}\cos\left(\dfrac{m}{2}\right)\cos\left(m+\dfrac{m}{2}\right)+\sqrt{60^2-\left[\dfrac{2e}{R}\cos\left(\dfrac{m}{2}\right)\sin\left(m+\dfrac{m}{2}\right)\right]^2}$}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L013}{}{quae est \textit{distantia centri epicycli a centro aequantis.} Eam \textgreek{Δ} vocemus.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0693-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L014}{}{\Indent Nunc ad rectam HE′, quantum necesse est productam, perpendicularis EN ducatur. In}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L015}{15}{triangulo rectangulo E′NE:}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L016}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{10.80mm}$NE=E'E\,\dfrac{\sin NE'E}{R}=\dfrac{e\sin m}{R}$\,,}\rlap{\hspace*{84.40mm}$E'N=E'E\,\dfrac{\sin E'EN}{R}=\dfrac{e\cos m}{R}$\,;}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L017}{}{ergo in triangulo HNE:}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L018}{}{\textit{distantia geocentrica centri epicycli} $=HE=\sqrt{(E'H+E'N)^2+\overline{NE}^2}$}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L019}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{56.10mm}$=\sqrt{\left(\Delta+\dfrac{e\cos m}{R}\right)^2+\left(\dfrac{e\sin m}{R}\right)^2}$\,.}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L020}{20}{Et in eodem triangulo HNE:}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L021}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{13.80mm}sin \textit{aequat. centri} $=$ sin $E'HE=R\,\dfrac{NE}{HE}=\dfrac{e\sin m}{\text{dist.\ geocentr.\ centri epicycli}}$\,.}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L022}{}{Supputatio autem \textit{aequationis anomaliae} (seu anguli HEY = arcus XY) eodem modo quo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L023}{}{in reliquis planetis fit. \textit{Tabulae Mercurii} sicut in reliquis planetis docui institutae sunt.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0693-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L024}{}{\Indent Tabularum omnium elementa, tam in sexagesimis radii deferentis 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} sumpti quam in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L025}{25}{decimalibus eiusdem radii 1\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} facti, haec sunt:}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L026}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{50.10mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{{\fontsize{9.5}{11}\selectfont Excentricitas aequantis (1)}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{111.30mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{{\fontsize{9.5}{11}\selectfont Radius epicycli in distantia media.}}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L027}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{20.90mm}\smash{\rlap{Saturno}}}\rlap{\hspace*{44.10mm}\smash{\llap{6}\rlap{\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{52.40mm}\smash{\llap{50′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{54.60mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{=}}}\rlap{\hspace*{57.40mm}\smash{\rlap{0.113888 ...}}}\rlap{\hspace*{95.90mm}\smash{\llap{6}\rlap{\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{103.30mm}\smash{\llap{30′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{106.00mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{=}}}\rlap{\hspace*{108.90mm}\smash{\rlap{0.108333 ...}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L028}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{20.90mm}\smash{\rlap{Iovi}}}\rlap{\hspace*{44.10mm}\smash{\llap{5}\rlap{\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{52.40mm}\smash{\llap{30′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{54.60mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{=}}}\rlap{\hspace*{57.40mm}\smash{\rlap{0.091666 ...}}}\rlap{\hspace*{95.90mm}\smash{\llap{11}\rlap{\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{103.30mm}\smash{\llap{30′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{106.00mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{=}}}\rlap{\hspace*{108.90mm}\smash{\rlap{0.191666 ...}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L029}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{20.90mm}\smash{\rlap{Marti}}}\rlap{\hspace*{44.10mm}\smash{\llap{12}\rlap{\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{52.40mm}\smash{\llap{0′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{54.60mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{=}}}\rlap{\hspace*{57.40mm}\smash{\rlap{0.2}}}\rlap{\hspace*{95.90mm}\smash{\llap{39}\rlap{\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{103.30mm}\smash{\llap{30′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{106.00mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{=}}}\rlap{\hspace*{108.90mm}\smash{\rlap{0.658333 ...}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L030}{30}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{20.90mm}\smash{\rlap{Veneri}}}\rlap{\hspace*{44.10mm}\smash{\llap{2}\rlap{\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{52.40mm}\smash{\llap{30′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{54.60mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{=}}}\rlap{\hspace*{57.40mm}\smash{\rlap{0.041666 ...}}}\rlap{\hspace*{95.90mm}\smash{\llap{43}\rlap{\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{103.30mm}\smash{\llap{10′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{106.00mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{=}}}\rlap{\hspace*{108.90mm}\smash{\rlap{0.719444 ...}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-body-L031}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{20.90mm}\smash{\rlap{Mercurio}}}\rlap{\hspace*{44.10mm}\smash{\llap{3}\rlap{\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{52.40mm}\smash{\llap{0′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{54.60mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{=}}}\rlap{\hspace*{57.40mm}\smash{\rlap{0.05}}}\rlap{\hspace*{95.90mm}\smash{\llap{22}\rlap{\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{103.30mm}\smash{\llap{30′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{106.00mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{=}}}\rlap{\hspace*{108.90mm}\smash{\rlap{0.375}}}}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}{\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0693-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0693-notes-L001}{}{\Indent (1) Quae Veneri et Superioribus est duplum excentricitatis deferentis.}
}
\DSource{0694}{245}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0694-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L001}{}{\Indent Eadem in \textit{Canopi inscriptione,} anno 10 Antonini (seu annos fere sex post Almagestum)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L002}{}{condita, quae radices vel fundamenta (\textgreek{ἀρχαί}) caelestium motuum praebet, et ad calcem Hy-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L003}{}{pothesium plerumque adiungitur (ed. Halma p. 57–58); in \textit{Hypothesibus} autem, inscriptione}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L004}{}{illa serioribus, radius epicycli Mercurii 22\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 15′ = 0.3708333 ..., excentricitas aequantis Sa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L005}{5}{turni 6\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 40′ = 0.11111 ... statuuntur (1).}
\par\vspace{5.7mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0694-H06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L006}{}{\CenterLine{\textit{Ad pag. 138-139.} — \textbf{Stationes planetarum.}}}
\par\vspace{4.6mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0694-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L007}{}{\Indent De his tabulis cfr. cap. XLVI (t. I, p. 114) et adp. spuriam D (t. I, p. 147).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0694-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L008}{}{\Indent A \textit{Ptolemaicis} XII, 7 (ed. Halma t. II, p. 354–55) propterea in minutis discrepant, quod}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L009}{}{\Name{Ptolemaeus} non \textit{centrum verum} sed \textit{medium} (\textgreek{τὸ περιοδικὸν μῆκος}) tanquam tabularum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L010}{10}{argumentum sumpsit. Sed congruunt cum \Name{Theonis} t. III, p. 11–15, quo collato facile fuit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L011}{}{numeros codicis probare (2). Animadvertendum etiam est summam stationum semper 360° esse;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L012}{}{nam, ut e sequentibus patebit, si 180° — \textgreek{ψ} est anomalia aequata priorem stationem seu initium}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L013}{}{regressus efficiens, 180° + \textgreek{ψ} erit anomalia aequata alteram stationem seu finem regressus gignens.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0694-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L014}{}{\Indent Breviter doctrinam Ptolemaicam stationum (\textgreek{στηριγμοί}) et regressuum (\textgreek{προηγήσεις}) planetarum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L015}{15}{exponam (\textit{Almag.} XII, 1–7, ed. Halma t. II, p. 312–355), ut ratio tabularum supputandarum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L016}{}{cognoscatur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0694-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L017}{}{\Indent E centro Terrae linea recta ad situm planetae in circumferentia epicycli ducatur; protra-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L018}{}{haturque ita ut epicyclum secet. Quotiescumque ratio dimidiae rectae epicyclo contentae ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L019}{}{partem rectae exteriorem maior est ratione velocitatis epicycli (in circumferentia deferentis)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L020}{20}{ad velocitatem planetae (in circumferentia epicycli) planeta \textit{retrogradus} est; quotiescumque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L021}{}{vero hae rationes aequantur, planeta est \textit{stationarius.}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0694-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L022}{}{\Indent Vidimus (p. 238) centrum epicycli aequabili motu moveri circa punctum quoddam di-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L023}{}{versum a centro Terra et a centro deferentis. Elongatio media centri epicycli a longitudine}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L024}{}{apogei, sive centrum medium (p. 240), sit \textit{m}; quaeritur quantum motu vero respectu centri}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L025}{25}{Terrae moveatur epicyclus dum motu medio parvum angulum \textit{a} perambulat. Supputentur ae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L026}{}{quationes centri respondentes quantitatibus \textit{m} + \textit{a} et \textit{m}; aequationum differentia subtra-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L027}{}{hatur aut addatur, pro opportunitate, arcui \textit{a}; exibit arcus motu vero ab epicycli centro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L028}{}{confectus, seu \textit{velocitas vera epicycli} tunc temporis. Qua cognita, tempus \textit{t} respondens medio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L029}{}{motui \textit{a} e tabulis eruatur; facile tunc erit medium motum anomaliae (seu planetae in epicyclo)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L030}{30}{correspondentem deducere, cui addendum aut subtrahendum erit quod subtractum aut additum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L031}{}{est arcui \textit{a} (3); exibit \textit{velocitas vera planetae} in epicyclo.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0694-P07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L032}{}{\Indent Nunc quantum, in initio regressus aut initio progressus, planeta ab apogeo vero epicycli distat}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L033}{}{cognoscere oportet. Ex centro E Terrae, linea recta epicyclum secans duobus punctis T et K}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L034}{}{hac ratione ducatur, ut $\dfrac{\mathrm{TK}}{2}$ (seu LT) sit ad TE sicut velocitas epicycli ad velocitatem pla-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L035}{35}{netae; erit igitur T punctum epicycli in quo planeta stationarius cernitur et incipit regredi.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-body-L036}{}{Ducatur HL, necessario perpendicularis ad TK.}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0694-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) Apud \Name{Tannery}, \textit{Recherches,} p. 251, qui}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-notes_left-L002}{}{Hypotheses sequitur, emendentur radius epicycli}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-notes_left-L003}{}{Veneris (false 0.71777...), Martis (false 0.65333...),}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-notes_left-L004}{}{et excentricitas aequantis Iovis (false 0.09666...).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0694-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-notes_left-L005}{}{\Indent (2) Sed apud \Name{Theonem} perperam I statio Iovis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-notes_left-L006}{}{sub 42° et 54° argumenti est 124° \textit{21}′ et 128° \textit{46}′}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-notes_right-L001}{}{loco 124° \textit{28}′ et 128° \textit{43}′ ut recte \Name{al-Battānī} habet.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-notes_right-L002}{}{Praeterea, \Name{Schiaparelli} monente, falsa erant mi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-notes_right-L003}{}{nuta stationum Martis sub 90° apud \Name{Theonem} et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-notes_right-L004}{}{codicem \Name{Escurialensem} (25′ et 35′ loco iusto-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-notes_right-L005}{}{rum 22′ et 38′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0694-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0694-notes_right-L006}{}{\Indent (3) I. e. addendum quod antea subtractum erat etc.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0695}{246}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0695-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L001}{}{\Indent Ob constructiones factas nota est ratio LT ad TE, ergo et 2LT (seu KT) ad TE, et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L002}{}{KE ad TE. — Recta HD nota est, quia radius epicycli; HE, quia distantia centri epicycli a}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L003}{}{centro Terrae, supputanda regulis alibi a me expositis; fiunt igitur notae PE ac DE. Sed est (1)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L004}{}{PE × DE = KE × TE; vidimus autem rationem KE ad TE notam esse; qua re etiam sin-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L005}{5}{gulae KE, TE innotescent, unde quoque KT, eius dimidia pars LT, et LE.}
\par\vspace{2mm}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.123\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0695-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0695-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.857\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.143\FullSourceWidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L006}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{21.00mm}Angulus LHT notus ex regula\quad sin $LHT=R\,\dfrac{LT}{HT}$\,,}}
\par\vspace{2.2mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L007}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{21.60mm}angulus HEL notus ex regula\quad cos $HEL=R\,\dfrac{LE}{HE}$\,;}}
\par\vspace{1.2mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L008}{}{noti igitur ang. LHE = 90° — HEL, ang. THD = LHE — LHT, et arcus TD}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L009}{}{quaesitus ut innotesceret epicycli punctum in quo planeta esset stationarius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L010}{10}{regressumque inciperet si \textit{centrum epicycli non moveretur.} — Ad cogno-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L011}{}{scendam alteram stationem et initium progressus sufficit in dextera parte}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L012}{}{epicycli omnia repetere quae in sinistra exposui; patebit quoque, \textit{situ epi-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L013}{}{\textit{cycli non mutato,} initium regressus et initium progressus aequaliter ab}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L014}{}{apogeo vero epicycli distare.}
\end{minipage}\par
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0695-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L015}{15}{\Indent Quaeritur nunc \textit{verus} epicycli arcus quem planeta ab initio ad medium retrogradationis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L016}{}{perambulat.}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{102.50mm}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0695-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L017}{}{\Indent Sit E centrum Terrae, E′ aequantis, H epicycli; ergo P}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L018}{}{apogeum epicycli verum, D perigeum epicycli verum, Q apo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L019}{}{geum epicycli medium. Sit T planeta in eo epicycli puncto in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L020}{20}{quo regredi incipit. Arcus TD iam e praecedentibus notus;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L021}{}{eum tamen planeta in dimidia parte regressus praecise non}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L022}{}{absolvit, nam, accedente planeta in epicyclo ad perigeum ve-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L023}{}{rum D, centrum epicycli ab apogeo deferentis amplius elonga-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L024}{}{tur. Hoc est, in medio verae retrogradationis centrum epicycli}
\end{minipage}\hspace{2mm}\begin{minipage}[t]{83.50mm}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0695-body-F02}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0695-F02}\end{minipage}\par
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L025}{25}{in \textit{h,} perigeum verum epicycli in \textit{d} erit; ergo verus retrogradationis arcus Td, qui prope in-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L026}{}{notescet cum noto angulo DET subtrahatur angulus quem linea veri motus epicycli describit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L027}{}{tempore dimidiae retrogradationis suppositae TD. Medius motus epicycli hoc temporis spatio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L028}{}{directe e tabulis elicitur; sitque \textit{n.} Accipiantur aequationes centri respondentes centro medio \textit{m,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L029}{}{antea memorato, et quantitati \textit{m} + \textit{n}; aequationes repertae suis mediis centris addantur aut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L030}{30}{subducantur, pro opportunitate; exibunt centra vera, quorum differentia erit arcus ab epicyclo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L031}{}{motu vero absolutus tempore dimidiae retrogradationis TD. Arcus ille dematur de noto an-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L032}{}{gulo DET; prope relinquetur verus arcus retrogradationis quaesitus, cuius duplum erit fere}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L033}{}{verus arcus a planeta contra successionem signorum descriptus tempore totius regressus.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L034}{}{Solutio quidem adproximata, sed sufficiens.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0695-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L035}{35}{\Indent Inde constructio tabularum patet. Via nunc descripta, \Name{Ptolemaeus} priorem et alteram}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L036}{}{stationem quaerit pro maxima, media, minima distantia centri epicycli a centro Terrae. Sit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L037}{}{I differentia inter maximam et mediam distantiam epicycli, II differentia inter distantiam centri}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L038}{}{epicycli in quodam dato deferentis puncto et distantiam mediam, III differentia inter stationem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L039}{}{pro epicyclo apogeum deferentis occupante et stationem pro epicyclo in media distantia; ponit tunc}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-body-L040}{40}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.80mm}statio pro dato epicycli situ in deferente $=$ statio pro media distantia $\pm\,\dfrac{\mathit{II}\times\mathit{III}}{\mathit{I}}$\,.}}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0695-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) Si ex puncto extra circulum ducantur duae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-notes_left-L002}{}{secantes, rectangulum contentum altera secante ip-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-notes_left-L003}{}{siusque portione exteriore aequabitur rectangulo con-}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-notes_right-L001}{}{tento altera secante ipsiusque portione exteriore (co-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0695-notes_right-L002}{}{rollarium prop. 36\textsuperscript{ae} libri III \Name{Euclidis}).}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0696}{247}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L001}{}{Similiter procedens quoque in iis epicycli situbus qui inter mediam et minimam distantiam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L002}{}{centri epicycli a Terra sunt, stationes ad senos gradus elongationis \textit{mediae} (1) epicycli ab}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L003}{}{apogeo deferentis constituit. Quo in faciendo ponit stationes illas crescere aut decrescere pro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L004}{}{portionaliter ad decrementum aut incrementum distantiae geocentricae epicycli respectu di-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L005}{5}{stantiae mediae; quod, licet verum non sit, insensibilem tamen errorem gignit. — Binae co-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L006}{}{lumnae in tabulis singulis planetis assignatae exhibent anomalias veras seu aequatas (\textgreek{αἱ τῆς}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L007}{}{\textgreek{διευκρινημένης ἀνωμαλίας ἀποχαὶ ἀπὸ τῶν φαινομένων ἀπογείων τῶν ἐπικύκλων}) priorem alteramque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L008}{}{stationem gignentes.}
\par\vspace{4.2mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0696-H09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L009}{}{\CenterLine{\textit{Ad pag. 140-141.} — \textbf{Latitudines planetarum.}}}
\par\vspace{3.6mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0696-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L010}{10}{\Indent De his tabulis agit auctor cap. XLVII (t. I, p. 115–116); sumptae ex \textit{Almagesto} XIII, 5}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L011}{}{(ed. Halma, t. II, p. 412–13). In edit. Halma obliquitas Mercurii sub 30° vel 330° perperam 45′}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L012}{}{loco 55′ est; de minutis autem maximae latitudinis australis Saturni (ed. Halma 3° 4′) videas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L013}{}{t. I, p. 116, adn. 8. — In ultima columna pag. 141 \textit{gradus} et \textit{minuta} scripsi, codice Escuria-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L014}{}{lensi fretus; sed \textit{minuta} et \textit{secundae} sunt.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0696-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L015}{15}{\Indent De inscriptione ad dexterum latus pag. 140, egi t. I, p. 282.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0696-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L016}{}{\Indent Doctrinam latitudinum planetarum, summopere perplexam, \Name{Ptolemaeus} XIII, 1-6}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L017}{}{(ed. Halma t. II, p. 367–416) perobscure exponit; eandem nunc breviter docere conabor, ut ta-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L018}{}{bularum constructio pateat.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0696-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L019}{}{\Indent A \textit{tribus superioribus planetis} ordiamur, quorum latitudo ex duabus partibus constat:}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L020}{20}{inclinatione plani deferentis respectu plani eclipticae, et inclinatione plani epicycli ad planum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L021}{}{deferentis (2). Immutabilis prior; variabilis altera, nam epicycli apogeum (et perigeum) verum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L022}{}{quemdam circellum (\textgreek{κυκλίσκος}) perpendicularem ad planum deferentis, in cuius circumferentia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L023}{}{centrum suum habet, hac lege describit: quod semicircumferentiam circelli absolvit dum epicycli}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L024}{}{centrum ab altero nodorum (seu ab altero intersectionis locorum deferentis et plani eclipticae)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L025}{25}{ad alterum pervenit; unde consequitur apogeum verum (et perigeum) inaequali motu circellum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L026}{}{perambulare, et revolutiones motu aequabili non circa proprium circelli centrum absolvere sed}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L027}{}{circa aliud punctum circumferentiae deferentis quod a circelli centro eadem ratione distet qua}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L028}{}{centrum deferentis a centro Terrae (3). Centro epicycli in nodis exstante, linea apogei perigeique}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L029}{}{epicycli cum plano simul deferentis et eclipticae coincidit; extra nodos apogea epicycli semper}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L030}{30}{inter planum deferentis planumque eclipticae consistunt, perigea ultra planum deferentis. Hinc}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L031}{}{elucet maximam latitudinem contingere planetis superioribus in perigeo vero epicycli.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0696-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L032}{}{\Indent In plano ad planum eclipticae perpendiculari sit EB communis horum planorum sectio,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L033}{}{EH eiusdem plani perpendicularis et plani deferentis sectio, DHA sectio plani perpendicularis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L034}{}{et plani epicycli, E centrum Terrae et eclipticae, H centrum epicycli ZDGA. Planum epicycli}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L035}{35}{ita collocetur ut omnes rectae in epicycli superficie ad diametrum DA perpendiculariter ductae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-body-L036}{}{sint parallelae ad planum eclipticae (4). Sit A apogeum verum epicycli, T locus planetae in}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0696-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) \textit{Verae} in tabulis \Name{Theonis} et \Name{al-Battānī.}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0696-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-notes_left-L002}{}{\Indent (2) Numerantur in circulo maximo perpendi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-notes_left-L003}{}{culari ad planum eclipticae per cuius polos transit;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-notes_left-L004}{}{\textit{Almag.} XIII, 3 (ed. Halma, t. II, p. 375–76).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0696-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-notes_left-L005}{}{\Indent (3) \textit{Almag.} XIII, 2 (ed. Halma, t. II, p. 373): \textgreek{τὰς}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-notes_left-L006}{}{\textgreek{μέντοι πρὸς ὁμαλὴν κίνησιν περιφορὰς οὐ περὶ τὸ ἴδιον}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-notes_left-L007}{}{\textgreek{κέντρον ἔχουσιν ἀποτελουμένας, περί τι δὲ ἕτερον τὸ}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-notes_left-L008}{}{\textgreek{ποιῆσον τὴν αὐτὴν ἐκκεντρότητα πρὸς τὸν κυκλίσκον τῇ}}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-notes_right-L001}{}{\textgreek{κατὰ μῆκος τοῦ ἀστέρος πρὸς τὸν διὰ μέσων τῶν ζωδίων}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-notes_right-L002}{}{\textgreek{κύκλον}.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0696-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-notes_right-L003}{}{\Indent (4) Ita faciendum, nam in Ptolemaicis latitu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-notes_right-L004}{}{dinum planetarum superiorum hypothesibus diametri}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-notes_right-L005}{}{quae ad angulos rectos secant diametros apogeorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-notes_right-L006}{}{ac perigeorum epicyclorum necessario semper sunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-notes_right-L007}{}{ad planum eclipticae parallelae; cfr. \textit{Almag.,} l. c.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0696-notes_right-L008}{}{(ed. Halma, t. II, p. 372).}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0697}{248}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L001}{}{epicyclo dato temporis momento; erit arcus AT anomalia vera seu aequata \textgreek{ψ}. Recta TK ducatur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L002}{}{perpendicularis ad DA (ergo parallela ad planum eclipticae); TL et KB demittantur perpen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L003}{}{diculares ad planum eclipticae; quadrilaterum TKBL manifeste rectangulum erit. Rectis BL,}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.294\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0697-body-F01}{}\hspace*{1.9mm}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0697-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.686\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.314\FullSourceWidth}
