\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L004}{}{ET, EL descriptis, angulus TEL erit \textit{latitudo} supputanda.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L005}{5}{Demum KN perpendicularis ad EH, et rectae TH, EK du-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L006}{}{cantur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0697-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L007}{}{\Indent In triangulo HKT, rectangulo in K, cognoscuntur an-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L008}{}{gulus THK (anomalia vera, \textgreek{ψ}) et latus HT (radius epi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L009}{}{cycli, \textit{r}); notae igitur fiunt}
\par\vspace{0.8mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L010}{10}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{11.40mm}$KT=\dfrac{r\sin\psi}{R}$\,,}\rlap{\hspace*{54.40mm}$KH=\dfrac{r\cos\psi}{R}$\,.}}
\par\vspace{0.8mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0697-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L011}{}{\Indent In triangulo HKN, rectangulo in N, notus est an-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L012}{}{gulus KHN seu epicycli inclinatio ad deferentem (\textit{i}); ergo}
\par\vspace{1mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L013}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{19.00mm}$KN=KH\,\dfrac{\sin i}{R}=\dfrac{r\cos\psi\sin i}{R^2}$\,,}}
\par\vspace{2mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L014}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{19.60mm}$HN=KH\,\dfrac{\cos i}{R}=\dfrac{r\cos\psi\cos i}{R^2}$\,.}}
\par\vspace{1mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0697-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L015}{15}{\Indent Et quia situs centri epicycli in deferente datus cen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L016}{}{setur, nota erit, per formulam pag. 240,}
\par\vspace{1mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L017}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}$NE=EH\mp HN=$ dist. geocentr. epicycli $\mp\,\dfrac{r\cos\psi\cos i}{R^2}$\,.}}
\end{minipage}\par
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L018}{}{In triangulo rectangulo KNE sunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L019}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{14.00mm}$KE=\sqrt{\overline{NE}^2+\overline{KN}^2}=\sqrt{\left(\text{dist.\ epic.}\mp\dfrac{r\cos\psi\cos i}{R^2}\right)^2+\left(\dfrac{r\cos\psi\sin i}{R^2}\right)^2}$}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L020}{20}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{13.70mm}sin $KEN=R\,\dfrac{KN}{KE}=\dfrac{r\cos\psi\sin i}{R\sqrt{\left(\text{dist.\ epic.}\mp\dfrac{r\cos\psi\cos i}{R^2}\right)^2+\left(\dfrac{r\cos\psi\sin i}{R^2}\right)^2}}$\,.}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L021}{}{Unde colliguntur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L022}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{20.90mm}BEK = KEN + BEH = KEN + inclinatio deferentis ad eclipticam,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L023}{}{et, quia triangulum KBE ob factas constructiones est rectangulum in B,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L024}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{26.10mm}$KB=KE\,\dfrac{\sin BEK}{R}$\,,}\rlap{\hspace*{88.50mm}$EB=KE\,\dfrac{\cos BEK}{R}$\,.}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L025}{25}{In triangulo LBE, rectangulo in B, est\quad $EL=\sqrt{\overline{EB}^2+\overline{BL}^2}$\,,\quad ubi etiam BL nota, quia ae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L026}{}{qualis opposito lateri KT (quadrilateri rectanguli TKBL) antea supputato; ob constructiones}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L027}{}{factas angulus ELT rectus est, ergo\quad $ET=\sqrt{\overline{EL}^2+\overline{LT}^2}$\,,\quad et innotescet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L028}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{41.20mm}sin latit. $=$ sin $TEL=R\,\dfrac{LT}{ET}=R\,\dfrac{KB}{ET}$\,.}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0697-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L029}{}{\Indent Hac via, quam supputationi trigonometricae accommodavi, \Name{Ptolemaeus} pro senis ac}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L030}{30}{ternis anomaliae verae gradibus deduxit latitudines maximas planetarum \textit{superiorum,} quae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L031}{}{fiunt in limite boreali deferentis (col. I cuiuscumque horum planetarum) et in australi (col. II}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L032}{}{eorumdem). Ad latitudines in locis intermediis deferentis supputandas adhibuit partes propor-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-body-L033}{}{tionales, de quibus infra dicendum erit (1).}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0697-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) \textit{Limes borealis,} \textgreek{τὸ βόρειον πέρας}, est pun-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-notes_left-L002}{}{ctum deferentis maxime in septentriones declinans,}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-notes_right-L001}{}{90° gradibus igitur ab utroque nodo in boream di-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0697-notes_right-L002}{}{stans; \textit{limes australis,} \textgreek{τὸ νότιον πέρας}, punctum de-}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0698}{249}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0698-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L001}{}{\Indent Ut calculus simplicior et ad nostras tabulas logarithmicas aptior fiat, ita operandum est:}
\par\vspace{1mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L002}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}(1)}\rlap{\hspace*{34.10mm}tang\enspace $KEN=R\,\dfrac{KN}{NE}=R\,\dfrac{r\cos\psi\sin i}{R^2\,\text{dist.\ epic.}\mp r\cos\psi\cos i}$}}
\par\vspace{1.6mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L003}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{32.20mm}$BEK=KEN+BEH=KEN+$ inclinat. deferentis}}
\par\vspace{1.6mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L004}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{48.30mm}$KE=R\,\dfrac{KN}{\sin KEN}=\dfrac{r\cos\psi\sin i}{R\sin KEN}$}}
\par\vspace{1.6mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L005}{5}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{40.30mm}$EB=KE\,\dfrac{\cos BEK}{R}=\dfrac{r\cos\psi\sin i\cos BEK}{R^2\sin KEN}$}}
\par\vspace{1.6mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L006}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{22.90mm}tang\enspace $BEL=R\,\dfrac{BL}{EB}$\,,}\rlap{\hspace*{63.10mm}sed}\rlap{\hspace*{75.50mm}$BL=KT=\dfrac{r\sin\psi}{R}$\,,}\rlap{\hspace*{115.10mm}ergo (1)}}
\par\vspace{1.6mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L007}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}(2)}\rlap{\hspace*{28.90mm}tang $BEL=R\,\dfrac{r\sin\psi\,R^2\sin KEN}{R\,r\cos\psi\sin i\cos BEK}=R\,\dfrac{\text{tang}\,\psi\sin KEN}{\sin i\cos BEK}$\,.}}
\par\vspace{1.6mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L008}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}Cognitis}\rlap{\hspace*{40.00mm}$LT=KB=EB\,\dfrac{\sin BEK}{\cos BEK}=\dfrac{EB\,\text{tang}\,BEK}{R}$}}
\par\vspace{1.6mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L009}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}et}\rlap{\hspace*{43.10mm}$EL=R\,\dfrac{EB}{\cos BEL}$\,,\enspace statim innotescet}}
\par\vspace{1.6mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L010}{10}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}(3)}\rlap{\hspace*{27.40mm}tang latit. $=$ tang\enspace $TEL=R\,\dfrac{LT}{EL}=\dfrac{\cos BEL\,\text{tang}\,BEK}{R}$\,.}}
\par\vspace{1.5mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0698-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L011}{}{\Indent Tribus formulis 1, 2, 3 igitur \textit{latitudines planetarum superiorum} computantur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0698-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L012}{}{\Indent Ad \textit{planetas inferiores} (Venerem et Mercurium) nunc pergamus. Tres inaequalitates in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L013}{}{latitudine sortiuntur, quas singillatim enumerabo:}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0698-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L014}{}{\Indent\Indent I\textsuperscript{a} inaequalitas est deferentis ad planum eclipticae inclinatio (\textgreek{ἔγκλισις τοῦ ἐκκέντρου}, apud}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L015}{15}{nonnullos nostratum \textit{deviatio}); eaque non ut in superioribus fixa, sed variabilis ad verum centri}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L016}{}{epicycli cursum (2). Nulla enim, centro epicycli alterutrum nodum (seu communem planorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L017}{}{deferentis et eclipticae sectionem) occupante, crescit postea gradatim cum epicyclus a nodis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L018}{}{recedat, Venerem semper in boream ferens, Mercurium semper in austrum; maximam attingit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L019}{}{quantitatem (10′ ad boream in Venere, 45′ ad austrum in Mercurio) in apogeo aut perigeo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L020}{20}{deferentis. Quoniam epicyclus in anno sidereo circumferentiam deferentis conficit, patet ex}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L021}{}{dictis apogeum deferentis dimidio anno esse boreale, dimidio anno australe.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0698-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L022}{}{\Indent\Indent II\textsuperscript{a} inaequalitas (\textgreek{ἔγκλισις τοῦ ἐπικύκλου} « inclinatio epicycli », Arabum \textit{mayl falak at-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L023}{}{\textit{-tadwīr,} nostratum \textit{inclinatio} vel \textit{declinatio}) est inclinatio illius diametri epicycli, quae apogeum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L024}{}{ac perigeum verum epicycli coniungit, ad planum deferentis. Apogeum et perigeum epicycli}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L025}{25}{circellum quidem describunt perpendicularem ad planum deferentis, sicut in superioribus planetis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L026}{}{vidimus; sed hac differentia quod inclinatio Veneri et Mercurio maxima fit in nodis, nulla in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L027}{}{apogeo aut perigeo deferentis, nam in apogeo et perigeo deferentis diametrus illa epicycli in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L028}{}{plano deferentis iacet. In apogeo deferentis verum apogeum epicycli Veneris ad boream de-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L029}{}{ferentis incipit vergere, perigeum ad austrum; res contraria Mercurio contingit. In perigeo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-body-L030}{30}{deferentis apogeum verum epicycli Veneris ad austrum, perigeum ad boream deferentis vergere}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-notes_left-L001}{}{ferentis oppositum. In Marte limes borealis est quoque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-notes_left-L002}{}{apogeum deferentis, limes australis perigeum; non}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-notes_left-L003}{}{ita vero in Saturno et Iove. Saturni limes borealis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-notes_left-L004}{}{50° ab apogeo deferentis in occasum versus distat;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-notes_left-L005}{}{Iovis 20° ab apogeo in ortum; quod iam pag. 239,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-notes_left-L006}{}{l. 8-10, vidimus.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0698-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-notes_right-L001}{}{\Indent (1) Quia\quad $R\,\dfrac{\sin\psi}{\cos\psi}=$ tang \textgreek{ψ}.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0698-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-notes_right-L002}{}{\Indent (2) \textit{Almag.} XIII, 1 (ed. Halma t. II, p. 370):}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-notes_right-L003}{}{\textgreek{Ὥστε καὶ ἐκ τούτου συνάγεσϑαι διότι αἱ μὲν τῶν ἐκκέν}-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-notes_right-L004}{}{\textgreek{τρων ἐγκλίσεις κινούμεναι καὶ αὐταὶ συναποκαϑίστανται}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0698-notes_right-L005}{}{\textgreek{ταῖς περιόδοις τῶν ἐπικύκλων}.}
\end{minipage}
\par\vspace{5mm}\hfill{\fontsize{8}{10}\selectfont 32}\hspace{10mm}
\DSource{0699}{250}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L001}{}{incipit; Mercurio res contraria evenit. — Situs nodi ascendentis Veneris non idem est ac Mer-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L002}{}{curii; Venus enim a nodo ascendente recedens est a parte apogei deferentis, Mercurius a}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L003}{}{parte perigei (1).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0699-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L004}{}{\Indent\Indent III\textsuperscript{a} inaequalitas (\textgreek{λόξωσις} « obliquitas », Arabum \textit{inḥirāf, warāb, iltiwā’,} nostratum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L005}{5}{\textit{obliquatio} vel \textit{reflexio}) est inclinatio variabilis illius diametri epicycli quae puncta mediarum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L006}{}{distantiarum coniungit, id est illius diametri quae ad angulos rectos diametrum per apogeum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L007}{}{et perigeum verum epicycli ductam secat. Talis diametri extremitates (90° — 270°) circellos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L008}{}{quoque describunt, sed perpendiculares ad planum eclipticae et habentes centra in diametris ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L009}{}{planum eclipticae parallelis. Haec centra eadem qua cetera velocitate moventur (\textgreek{ἰσοταχῶς τοῖς}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L010}{10}{\textgreek{ἄλλοις}). Haec obliquitas, ut secunda superiorum planetarum inclinatio, maxima est in apogeo aut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L011}{}{perigeo deferentis; nulla in nodis in quibus diametrus illa cum plano simul deferentis et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L012}{}{eclipticae coincidit. In apogeo deferentis, extremitas orientalis huius diametri epicycli Veneris}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L013}{}{est ad boream, in perigeo ad austrum; res contraria Mercurio contingit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0699-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L014}{}{\Indent E rebus nuper expositis satis colligitur omnia quae Veneri ad boream vergenti contingant,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L015}{15}{eadem et iisdem legibus Mercurio declinanti in austrum evenire.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0699-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L016}{}{\Indent In planetis superioribus vidimus latitudinem partim ex deferentis inclinatione, partim ex}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L017}{}{epicycli constare; ambas vero ita esse commixtas ut seorsum nunquam exsistant. Tres contra}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L018}{}{inaequalitates maximae Veneris et Mercurii singillatim facile possunt observationibus capi et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L019}{}{numeris supputari; planetae enim inferiores perigeum aut apogeum epicycli tunc occupant}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L020}{20}{cum centrum epicycli in apogeum aut perigeum deferentis incidit (2), qua re utroque loco ab}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L021}{}{ecliptica pariter in boream aut austrum declinantes apparent; praeterea cum epicycli inclinatio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L022}{}{maxima, nulla tunc obliquitas epicycli.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0699-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L023}{}{\Indent Tabularum constructio pro inferioribus planetis haec est. Maxima inclinatio diametri}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L024}{}{apogei–perigei epicycli, quam vidimus fieri cum epicycli centrum alterutrum deferentis nodum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L025}{25}{teneat, observationibus (3) comperta est 2° $\qfrac{1}{2}$ Veneri (4), 6° $\qfrac{1}{4}$ Mercurio (5). Centro epicycli}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L026}{}{semper in nodis constituto, quaeritur singulis aliis circumferentiae punctis quanta sit haec}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L027}{}{maxima \textit{inclinatio,} e centro Terrae visa.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0699-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L028}{}{\Indent In plano ad planum eclipticae perpendiculari sit EH amborum communis sectio, H epicycli}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L029}{}{centrum in alterutro nodo consistens, AD plani perpendicularis et plani epicycli sectio. Sit E}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L030}{30}{centrum Terrae eclipticaeque; erit EH distantia geocentrica epicycli tempore inclinationis maximae.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L031}{}{Circa H epicyclo DGAZ orthogonaliter ad planum illud perpendiculare descripto, diametrus GZ}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L032}{}{perpendicularis ad AD demittatur. Rectae omnes in superficie epicycli perpendiculariter ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L033}{}{AD ductae manifeste erunt parallelae ad eclipticae planum; recta autem GZ in ipsa super-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L034}{}{ficie eclipticae iacebit. — Cognoscuntur EH, AH (\textit{r,} epicycli radius), arcus AT seu angulus THA}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-body-L035}{35}{(\textgreek{ψ}, anomalia vera planetae T).}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0699-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) Haec vult \textit{Almag.} XIII, 2 (ed. Halma, t. II,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-notes_left-L002}{}{p. 373): \textgreek{καὶ ἔτι καὶ ἐπὶ τούτων ἡ τῆς ὁμοίας ἀφέσεως}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-notes_left-L003}{}{\textgreek{ἀρχή τε καὶ ἀποκατάστασις, ἐπὶ μὲν τοῦ τῆς Ἀφροδίτης}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-notes_left-L004}{}{\textgreek{ἀπὸ τοῦ κατὰ τὸ προσϑετικὸν ἡμικύκλιον συνδέσμου συ}-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-notes_left-L005}{}{\textgreek{νίσταται, ἐπὶ δὲ τοῦ τοῦ Ἑρμοῦ ἀπὸ τοῦ κατὰ τὸ ἀφαι}-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-notes_left-L006}{}{\textgreek{ρετικόν} « praeterea talis cursus initium et restitutio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-notes_left-L007}{}{« Veneri ex nodo semicirculi additivi fiunt, Mercurio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-notes_left-L008}{}{« ex nodo subtractivi ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0699-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-notes_left-L009}{}{\Indent (2) \textit{Almag.} XIII, 3 (ed. Halma, t. II, p. 376).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0699-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-notes_left-L010}{}{\Indent (3) \textit{Almag.} XIII, 3 (ed. Halma, t. II, p. 377-78).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0699-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-notes_left-L011}{}{\Indent (4) Haec est quantitas in \textit{centro epicycli;} alia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-notes_left-L012}{}{est inclinatio e \textit{Terra} visa. Lineis rectis iungantur}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.571\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0699-notes_right-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0699-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.409\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.591\FullSourceWidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-notes_right-L001}{}{cum Terrae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-notes_right-L002}{}{centro E cen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-notes_right-L003}{}{trum H epi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-notes_right-L004}{}{cycli, extre-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-notes_right-L005}{}{mitas apogea}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-notes_right-L006}{}{A diametri et}
\end{minipage}\par
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-notes_right-L007}{}{extremitas perigea P. Inclinatio 2° $\qfrac{1}{2}$ diametri sub-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-notes_right-L008}{}{tendet oculo observatoris terrestris angulum HEA}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-notes_right-L009}{}{1° 2′ in extremitate apogea, HEP 6° 22′ in perigea.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0699-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-notes_right-L010}{}{\Indent (5) Respectu \textit{centri Terrae} 1° 46′ in extremi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0699-notes_right-L011}{}{tate apogea, 4° 5′ in perigea.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0700}{251}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0700-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0700-body-L001}{}{\Indent Ducatur TK perpendicularis ad AH; item KL, TN perpendiculares ad planum eclipticae;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0700-body-L002}{}{exoritur quadrilaterum TKLN rectangulum. Ductis EN, ET, erit TEN angulus supputandus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0700-body-L003}{}{inclinationis puncti T epicycli. Demonstratio \Name{Ptolemaei} XIII, 4 (ed. Halma, t. II, p. 383–86)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0700-body-L004}{}{geometrica ac numeralis ita ad amussim trigonometrice redditur: In triangulo rectangulo THK}
\par\vspace{1.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0700-body-L005}{5}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{15.70mm}$TK=\dfrac{TH\times\sin THK}{R}=\dfrac{r\sin\psi}{R}$\,,}\rlap{\hspace*{81.30mm}$KH=\dfrac{TH\times\cos THK}{R}=\dfrac{r\cos\psi}{R}$\,;}}
\par\vspace{1.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0700-body-L006}{}{nota igitur LN = TK.}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{103.50mm}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0700-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0700-body-L007}{}{\Indent In triangulo rectangulo KHL, angulus KHL notus, quia maxi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0700-body-L008}{}{ma inclinatio \textit{i} diametri apogei-perigei ad deferentem; ergo}
\par\vspace{1mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0700-body-L009}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{16.00mm}$KL=TN=\dfrac{KH\times\sin KHL}{R}=\dfrac{r\cos\psi\sin i}{R^2}$}}
\par\vspace{1.6mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0700-body-L010}{10}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{20.90mm}$LH=\dfrac{KH\times\cos KHL}{R}=\dfrac{r\cos\psi\cos i}{R^2}$\,;}}
\par\vspace{1mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0700-body-L011}{}{itaque nota}
\par\vspace{1mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0700-body-L012}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{15.60mm}$EL=HE\mp LH=$ dist. epicycli $\mp\,\dfrac{r\cos\psi\cos i}{R^2}$}}
\par\vspace{1.6mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0700-body-L013}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{31.80mm}$EN=\sqrt{\overline{EL}^2+\overline{LN}^2}=$}}
\par\vspace{1.6mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0700-body-L014}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{13.40mm}$=\sqrt{\left(\text{dist.\ epic.}\mp\dfrac{r\cos\psi\cos i}{R^2}\right)^2+\left(\dfrac{r\sin\psi}{R}\right)^2}$\,.}}
\end{minipage}\hspace{2mm}\begin{minipage}[t]{82.50mm}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0700-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0700-F01}\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{1.6mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0700-body-L015}{15}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{1.60mm}$ET=\sqrt{\overline{EN}^2+\overline{TN}^2}=\sqrt{\left(\text{dist.\ epic.}\mp\dfrac{r\cos\psi\cos i}{R^2}\right)^2+\left(\dfrac{r\sin\psi}{R}\right)^2+\left(\dfrac{r\cos\psi\sin i}{R^2}\right)^2}$\,.}}
\par\vspace{1.6mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0700-body-L016}{}{Notus igitur angulus inclinationis quaesitus:\quad sin $TEN=R\,\dfrac{TN}{ET}$\,. Ita, pro senis ac ternis ano-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0700-body-L017}{}{maliae verae \textgreek{ψ} gradibus, supputat \Name{Ptolemaeus} priorem columnam planetarum inferiorum, quae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0700-body-L018}{}{maximam inclinationem diametri apogei–perigei epicycli (in nodis deferentis contingentem)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0700-body-L019}{}{exhibet, qualis e Terrae centro cernitur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0700-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0700-body-L020}{20}{\Indent Simplicior calculus si tangentibus utamur:}
\par\vspace{1.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0700-body-L021}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}(1)}\rlap{\hspace*{29.00mm}tang\enspace $LEN=R\,\dfrac{LN}{EL}=R\,\dfrac{TK}{EL}=\dfrac{r\sin\psi}{\text{dist.\ epic.}\mp\dfrac{r\cos\psi\cos i}{R^2}}$}}
\par\vspace{1.6mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0700-body-L022}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{50.80mm}$EN=R\,\dfrac{LN}{\sin LEN}=\dfrac{r\sin\psi}{\sin LEN}$}}
\par\vspace{1.6mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0700-body-L023}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{14.00mm}tang\enspace $TEN=R\,\dfrac{TN}{EN}=R\,\dfrac{KL}{EN}=R\,\dfrac{\;\dfrac{r\cos\psi\sin i}{R^2}\;}{\dfrac{r\sin\psi}{\sin LEN}}=R\,\dfrac{r\cos\psi\sin i\sin LEN}{R^2\,r\sin\psi}$}}
\par\vspace{1.6mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0700-body-L024}{}{et, quoniam\quad $R\,\dfrac{\cos\psi}{\sin\psi}=$ cotg \textgreek{ψ},}
\par\vspace{1mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0700-body-L025}{25}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}(2)}\rlap{\hspace*{43.40mm}tang\enspace $TEN=\dfrac{\text{cotg}\,\psi\sin i\sin LEN}{R^2}$\,.}}
\par\vspace{2.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0700-body-L026}{}{Duabus igitur formulis (1) et (2) supputatio logarithmica ducitur.}
\DSource{0701}{252}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0701-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L001}{}{\Indent Nunc ad \textit{maximas obliquitates} diametri mediarum distantiarum pergamus, contingentes}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L002}{}{in apogeo et in perigeo deferentis. Observationibus et calculis comperit \Name{Ptolemaeus} (1)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L003}{}{maximam Veneris obliquitatem in apogeo deferentis 2° 27′, in perigeo 2° 34′; maximam Mer-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L004}{}{curii 2° 17′ in apogeo, 2° 46′ in perigeo (2). Unde colligitur in media distantia geocentrica epicycli}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L005}{5}{obliquitatem 2° $\qfrac{1}{2}$ proxime utrique planetae contingere; maximae enim discrepantiae ab hac}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L006}{}{quantitate media sunt\enspace — 3′ et + 4′ Veneri, — 13′ et + 16′ Mercurio. Tales variationes}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L007}{}{Veneris, adeo parvae ut instrumentis capi nequeant, omnino negligi possunt; itaque tabula pro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L008}{}{media epicycli obliquitate 2° $\qfrac{1}{2}$ condita sufficiet ad omnes distantias geocentricas epicycli Ve-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L009}{}{neris. At variatio Mercurio contingens est $\qfrac{1}{4}$ gradus circiter, seu $\qfrac{1}{10}$ illorum 2° $\qfrac{1}{2}$; qua re}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L010}{10}{tabula, pro media distantia geocentrica instituta, emendatione quadam egebit pro reliquis di-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L011}{}{stantiis epicycli Mercurii.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0701-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L012}{}{\Indent Demonstrat \Name{Ptolemaeus} XIII, 4 (ed. Halma, t. II, p. 401–03) in quacumque distantia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L013}{}{geocentrica centri epicycli maximam obliquitatem accidere binis punctis contactus, seu punctis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L014}{}{quibus circumferentia epicycli tangitur a lineis tangentibus e centro Terrae productis. An-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L015}{15}{gulus in centro Terrae ab alterutra tangente et a recta per centra Terrae et epicycli ducta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L016}{}{contentus, est digressio vel elongatio maxima planetae a medio loco Solis, \textgreek{ἡ μεγίστη} [\textgreek{τῆς τοῦ}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L017}{}{\textgreek{ἡλίου μέσης παρόδου}] \textgreek{ἀπόστασις} seu \textgreek{διάστασις}: 46° circiter Veneri, 22° circiter Mercurio. His po-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L018}{}{sitis, haud difficile esset pro singulis epicycli punctis obliquitatem exacte supputare sicut e}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L019}{}{centro Terrae conspicitur, neglecta, utpote pro hoc instituto insensibili, inclinatione deferentis}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.170\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0701-body-F01}{}\hspace*{0.7mm}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0701-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.810\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.190\FullSourceWidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L020}{20}{ad eclipticam. Constructionibus nempe ad similes casus alibi adhibitis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L021}{}{ita \textit{potuisset} \Name{Ptolemaeus} supputationem ducere:}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0701-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L022}{}{\Indent Sit T situs planetae in epicyclo, Q epicycli apogeum verum; erit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L023}{}{arcus QH seu angulus QHT anomalia vera \textgreek{ψ}, quae nota censetur.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L024}{}{Recta HT nota, quia semidiametrus \textit{r} epicycli. Demittatur TN perpen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L025}{25}{dicularis ad QH; item TM perpendicularis ad planum deferentis seu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L026}{}{eclipticae et ad NM quae in hoc plano iacet. Notae erunt}
\par\vspace{1mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L027}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{17.10mm}$NT=\dfrac{r\sin\psi}{R}$}\rlap{\hspace*{70.40mm}$NH=\dfrac{r\cos\psi}{R}$\,,}}
\par\vspace{1mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L028}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}nota igitur tota}\rlap{\hspace*{28.30mm}$NE=NH+$ distantia geocentrica centri epicycli.}}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L029}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{2.30mm}$ET=\sqrt{\overline{NE}^2+\overline{NT}^2}=\sqrt{\left(\text{dist.\ epic.}\pm\dfrac{r\cos\psi}{R}\right)^2+\left(\dfrac{r\sin\psi}{R}\right)^2}$\,.}}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L030}{30}{Angulus TNM notus, quia maxima obliquitas \textit{o} plani epicycli ad de-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L031}{}{ferentis (3° $\qfrac{1}{2}$ Veneri, 7° Mercurio, ut adn. 2 huius pag. dixi), ergo}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L032}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{32.00mm}$MT=\dfrac{NT\sin TNM}{R}=\dfrac{r\sin\psi\sin o}{R^2}$\,.}}
\par\vspace{1mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L033}{}{Quoniam MT est perpendicularis ad planum deferentis seu eclipticae,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L034}{}{necessario perpendicularis et ad EM erit, centrum E Terrae iungentem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L035}{35}{puncto M plani deferentis; angulus igitur TME rectus, et}
\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L036}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.60mm}sin obliq. $=$ sin\enspace $TEM=R\,\dfrac{MT}{ET}=\dfrac{r\sin\psi\sin o}{R\sqrt{\left(\text{dist.\ epic.}\pm\dfrac{r\cos\psi}{R}\right)^2+\left(\dfrac{r\sin\psi}{R}\right)^2}}$\,.}}
\par\vspace{1mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L037}{}{Simplicior calculus per tangentes. Differentiam longitudinis inter centrum epicycli et planeta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-body-L038}{}{consideremus, quae est angulus in plano \textit{eclipticae} (vel deferentis) contentus recta EN et}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0701-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) \textit{Almag.} XIII, 3 (Halma, II, 376) ac potissi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-notes_left-L002}{}{mum XIII, 4 (Halma II, 407-10).}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0701-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-notes_right-L001}{}{\Indent (2) Sunt obliquitates e centro Terrae visae;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0701-notes_right-L002}{}{in centro epicycli 3° $\qfrac{1}{2}$ Veneri, 7° Mercurio.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0702}{253}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L001}{}{\textit{proiectione} rectae TE per planetam ipsum transeuntis; consideremus scilicet angulum NEM,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L002}{}{trianguli in N rectanguli et habentis cathetum\quad $NM=$ proiectioni $NT'=\dfrac{r\sin\psi\cos o}{R^2}$\,.\enspace Erit}
\par\vspace{1.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L003}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}(1)}\rlap{\hspace*{11.20mm}tang\enspace $NEM=R\,\dfrac{NM}{NE}=R\,\dfrac{\;\dfrac{r\sin\psi\cos o}{R^2}\;}{\text{dist.\ epic.}\pm\dfrac{r\cos\psi}{R}}=\dfrac{r\sin\psi\cos o}{R\times\text{dist.\ epic.}\pm r\cos\psi}$\,.}}
\par\vspace{1.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L004}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{41.80mm}tang\enspace $TEM=$ tang obliquitatis $=R\,\dfrac{MT}{EM}$\,.}}
\par\vspace{1mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L005}{5}{Quoniam in triangulo NME, rectangulo in N, est\quad $EM=R\,\dfrac{NM}{\sin NEM}$\,,\quad erit}
\par\vspace{1.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L006}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{34.00mm}tang\enspace $TEM=R\,\dfrac{MT}{R\,\dfrac{NM}{\sin NEM}}=\dfrac{MT}{NM}\,\sin NEM$\,.}}
\par\vspace{1mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L007}{}{Sed in triangulo rectangulo NMT est\quad $MT:NM=\sin o:\cos o$\,;\quad ergo}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L008}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}(2)}\rlap{\hspace*{24.20mm}tang obliq. $=$ tang\enspace $TEM=\dfrac{\sin o}{\cos o}\,\sin NEM=\dfrac{\text{tang}\,o\,\sin NEM}{R}$\,.}}
\par\vspace{1mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0702-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L009}{}{\Indent Formulae (1) et (2) ad calculum logarithmicum sufficiunt.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0702-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L010}{10}{\Indent Sed \Name{Ptolemaeus}, ne nimium susciperet laborem, directam supputationem omisit aliamque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L011}{}{viam ingressus est. Absque demonstratione statuit XIII, 4 (ed. Halma, t. II, p. 410) maximas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L012}{}{digressiones (1) ad maximas obliquitates epicycli eandem habere rationem quam alia quaepiam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L013}{}{digressio seu elongatio ad obliquitatem puncti correspondentis epicycli. Ut res clarius eluceat,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L014}{}{sit E centrum Terrae, H centrum epicycli, EH communis sectio planorum eclipticae et epicycli.}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{111.00mm}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L015}{15}{Ducantur ED tangens ad epicyclum, et quaecumque secans EFN;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L016}{}{ex punctis F, D, N demittantur FI, DK, NM perpendiculares}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L017}{}{ad planum eclipticae; demum in eclipticae plano rectae EK, EIM}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L018}{}{describantur (2). Anguli DEK, NEM, FEI, sunt obliquitates}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L019}{}{(e centro Terrae visae) punctorum D, N, F epicycli, quorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L020}{20}{digressiones a Sole erunt anguli DEG, NEG, FEG. \Name{Ptole-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L021}{}{\Name{maeus} statuit}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L022}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{16.60mm}$DEG:DEK=NEG:NEM=FEG:FEI$}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L023}{}{seu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L024}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{3.20mm}maxima digressio : maxima obliquitas $=NEG:NEM$\,; etc.}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L025}{25}{Quod non exacte sed proxime verum est (3). Digressiones illae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L026}{}{manifeste idem sunt quod in theoria aequationum planetarum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L027}{}{\Name{Ptolemaeus} \textgreek{ἀνωμαλίας προσϑαφαίρεσις}, \Name{al-Battānī} « aequationem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L028}{}{anomaliae » vocaverunt; qua re tabulam obliquitatis facile}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L029}{}{condidit, ex correspondentibus aequationibus (in tabulis aequatio-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L030}{30}{num Veneris et Mercurii positis) sumens partem proportionalem}
\end{minipage}\hspace{2mm}\begin{minipage}[t]{75.00mm}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0702-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0702-F01}\end{minipage}\par
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-body-L031}{}{ad $\dfrac{46^\circ}{2^\circ 30'}$ pro Venere, ad $\dfrac{22^\circ}{2^\circ 30'}$ pro Mercurio.}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0702-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) Quas hoc capite \textgreek{μήκους προσϑαφαιρέσεις} vel}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-notes_left-L002}{}{\textgreek{κατὰ μῆκος προσϑαφαιρέσεις} appellat, nomen haud}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-notes_left-L003}{}{omnino recte e theoria aequationum planetarum su-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-notes_left-L004}{}{mens; cfr. paulum infra.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0702-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-notes_left-L005}{}{\Indent (2) Recta est linea EIM, quod puncta omnia}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-notes_right-L001}{}{E, I, M in communi sectione sunt plani eclipticae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-notes_right-L002}{}{planique ad angulos rectos eclipticam secantis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0702-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-notes_right-L003}{}{\Indent (3) Cfr. \Name{J. de Monte Regio} \textit{et} \Name{G. Purba-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-notes_right-L004}{}{\Name{chii} \textit{Epitome in Cl. Ptolemaei magnam composi-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0702-notes_right-L005}{}{\textit{tionem,} Basileae 1543, p. 259–60 (lib. XIII, prop. 19).}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0703}{254}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0703-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L001}{}{\Indent Ratione exposita qua binae columnae \textit{superiorum} et \textit{inferiorum} planetarum a \Name{Ptole-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L002}{}{\Name{maeo} supputatae sunt, ad explicandam columnam \textit{partium proportionalium} pergamus,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L003}{}{quae omnibus planetis est communis, ac t. II, p. 141 reperitur. Praecedentes columnae conditae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L004}{}{sunt pro centro epicycli aut in punctis maximarum latitudinum aut in nodis deferentis posito.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L005}{5}{Oportet columnas illas etiam ad puncta deferentis intermedia aptare. Supputatio directa nimis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L006}{}{longus est labor et permultas tabulas requirit; ad praecedentes igitur columnas pro quolibet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L007}{}{alio situ centri epicycli aptandas Ptolemaeus adhibuit partes proportionales. Eas tamen directe}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L008}{}{non supputavit; at considerans XIII, 4 (ed. Halma, II, p. 411) inclinationes obliquitatesque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L009}{}{eadem proxime ratione crescere aut decrescere qua latitudines Lunae, ex tabulis universas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L010}{10}{has latitudines lunares, exacte iam supputatas, accepit et in 12 multiplicavit ut sexagesimae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L011}{}{partes fierent; nam maxima Lunae latitudo est 5°, unde\enspace 5° × 12 = 60°.\enspace Facillime ita ul-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L012}{}{tima columna prodiit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0703-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L013}{}{\Indent Tabularum usum satis explicat \Name{al-Battānī} cap. XLVII (t. I, p. 115–16), cum quo potest}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L014}{}{\textit{Almag.} XIII, 6 (ed. Halma, t. II, p. 414–16) comparari. Longitudini verae Saturni 50° addendi,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L015}{15}{Iovis 20° demendi propterea sunt, quod linea absidum (seu apogei et perigei) deferentis in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L016}{}{Saturno 50° elongatur ab ortu lineae maximae inclinationis plani deferentis ad planum ecli-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L017}{}{pticae, in Iove 20° ab occasu. In reliquis planetis ambae lineae coincidunt. — Obliquitati Mer-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L018}{}{curii modo est addenda modo demenda ipsius decima pars, quod columna obliquitatis ad mediam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L019}{}{distantiam geocentricam centri epicycli condita est; variationes autem in distantia apogea aut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L020}{20}{perigea contingentes $\qfrac{1}{10}$ circiter sunt maximae obliquitatis 2° $\qfrac{1}{2}$ distantiae mediae, sicut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L021}{}{p. 252 dixi. — In supputanda inclinatione, longitudini verae Veneris 90° adduntur, Mercurii 270°,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L022}{}{quod inclinatio maxima in nodis fit, seu 90° et 270° elongationis ab apogeo deferentis. —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L023}{}{T. I, p. 116, iubet auctor Veneri $\qfrac{1}{6}$, Mercurio $\qfrac{8}{4}$ accipi partium sexagesimarum quas ex}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L024}{}{ultima columna sumpsimus cum iterum in tabulam cum longitudine intravimus. Id faciendum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L025}{25}{ne parva deferentis ad eclipticam inclinatio negligatur, quam vidimus $\dfrac{1^\circ}{10}$ Veneri, 45′ seu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L026}{}{$\dfrac{3^\circ}{4}$ Mercurio esse.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0703-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L027}{}{\Indent Quae \Name{Delambre}, \textit{Histoire de l’astron. ancienne,} t. II, p. 406, de tabulis latitudinum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L028}{}{Ptolemaicis iudicat, vix recta sunt; calculos enim non ad astronomi Graeci mentem duxit.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L029}{}{In gravi autem errore versatur cum scribit: « Ainsi pour l’obliquation de Mercure, il avertit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L030}{30}{« qu’ayant trouvé dans sa Table des erreurs\enspace — 13′ et + 16′, il avait corrigé sa Table à}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L031}{}{« raison de $\qfrac{1}{4}$ de degré, pour se rapprocher des observations. Cependant ses nombres cadrent}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L032}{}{« avec les miens; il n’a donc point exécuté ce changement qu’il annonce ». Manifeste non}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L033}{}{intellexit locum ed. Halma, t. II, p. 410, quo Ptolemaeus monet columnam illam supputatam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L034}{}{esse ad mediam distantiam geocentricam epicycli Mercurii, emendatione igitur proportionali}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L035}{35}{ad $\qfrac{1}{4}$ gradus egere pro distantiis apogeis et perigeis; cfr. paulum supra et p. 252.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0703-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L036}{}{\Indent Aliam, eamque longe simpliciorem omnibusque planetis communem, doctrinam latitudinum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L037}{}{occurrere in \textit{Hypothesibus} existimat \Name{Alwin Haebler}, \textit{Die Lehren des Claudius Pto-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L038}{}{\textit{lemaeus von den Bewegungen der Planeten,} in Zeitschr. für Mathematik und Physik,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-body-L039}{}{45. Bd., 1900, hist.-litter. Abteilung, p. 171, 192, 198 (1). Sed iniuria. In \textit{Hypothesibus} enim}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0703-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) Dissertatio tota pag. 161-198 periodici oc-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-notes_left-L002}{}{cupat. Primo considerationes generaliores de Graeca}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-notes_left-L003}{}{astronomia (p. 161-176) exhibet, acriter sed iure}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-notes_left-L004}{}{Halma arguens ob editiones versionesque librorum}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-notes_right-L001}{}{astronomicorum quas post Almagestum dedit (p. 167-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-notes_right-L002}{}{171). Doctrinam Ptolemaicam motuum Solis, Lunae,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-notes_right-L003}{}{planetarum in longitudine summatim p. 176-191}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0703-notes_right-L004}{}{adumbrat; monet postea Delambrii disquisitiones}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0704}{255}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L001}{}{(ed. Halma, p. 48–56), quae sunt Almagesto seriores, summatim pro singulis planetis sphaeras}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L002}{}{\Name{Ptolemaeus} recenset quibus eorum motus omnes repraesentantur. Circulum pareclipticum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L003}{}{(\textgreek{ὁ ὁμόκεντρος τῷ ζωδιακῷ κύκλος}) in zodiaci plano ab occasu ad ortum se eodem quo stellae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L004}{}{fixae motu revolventem, circulum obliquum (\textgreek{ὁ ἐγκεκλιμένος κύκλος, ὁ λοξός}), deferentem (\textgreek{ὁ ἔκκεν}-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L005}{5}{\textgreek{τρος}, Mercurio mobilem, reliquis fixum), centrum aequantis (\textgreek{περὶ ὃ κινεῖται ἰσοταχῶς}) describit,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L006}{}{sicut ego p. 238–39 ac 243 exposui; duabus parvis discrepantiis in Mercurio exceptis, quas p. 243,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L007}{}{adn. 1 et 2, memoravi. Peculiaritates autem epicyclorum pro singulis planetis ita enarrat: \textit{Cir-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L008}{}{\textit{cellus} (\textgreek{κυκλίσκος}) in epicycli sphaera, cui concentricus sit, censeatur, in obliqui plano iacens;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L009}{}{recta e centro aequantis per circelli centrum producta semper teneat bina circelli puncta quae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L010}{10}{apogeum et perigeum [epicycli] vocabimus; huius circelli magnitudo non determinatur. \textit{Cir-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L011}{}{\textit{cellus alter} sit, priori concentricus et in eodem obliqui plano, ita se aequabiliter revolvens}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L012}{}{circa centrum suum ut, ab apogeo [epicycli] incipiens, ab ortu ad occasum (\textgreek{ἐπὶ τὰ αὐτὰ τῇ}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L013}{}{\textgreek{τοῦ κόσμου περιστροφῇ} « in eandem partem in quam revolutio mundi ») moveatur eodem motu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L014}{}{quo centrum epicycli [circumferentiam aequantis] ambit; huius etiam circelli magnitudo non}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L015}{15}{memoratur. \textit{Tertius} demum \textit{circellus,} secundo primoque concentricus sed ad eorum planum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L016}{}{inclinatus censeatur, cuius circumferentiam planeta [motu suo anomalistico] aequabiliter ab}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L017}{}{occasu ad ortum (\textgreek{ἐπὶ τὰ ἐναντία τῇ τοῦ κόσμου περιστροφῇ}) ambiat; inclinatio et radius huius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L018}{}{tertii circelli pro singulis planetis indicantur. — Manifestum est tertium hunc circellum esse}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L019}{}{verum epicyclum; duos priores autem, sicut circulum pareclipticum, inutiles esse ad mathe-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L020}{20}{maticam rationem, et solummodo ad philosophicam caelestium sphaerarum inservire. Mani-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L021}{}{festum etiam tota hac \textit{Hypothesium} expositione nonnisi generalem adumbrationem motuum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L022}{}{planetarum contineri, \textit{nihil} vero eius quod ad supputandas \textit{latitudines} inserviat.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0704-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L023}{}{\Indent Monendum est elementa \textit{Hypothesium} non raro ab \textit{Almagesti} differre:}
\par\vspace{0.6mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L024}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{80.70mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{{\fontsize{9.5}{11}\selectfont Saturno}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{95.20mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{{\fontsize{9.5}{11}\selectfont Iovi}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{110.90mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{{\fontsize{9.5}{11}\selectfont Marti}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{125.80mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{{\fontsize{9.5}{11}\selectfont Veneri}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{141.60mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{{\fontsize{9.5}{11}\selectfont Mercurio}}}}}
\par\vspace{1mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L025}{25}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}\smash{\raisebox{-.55\baselineskip}{\rlap{Inclinatio epicycli ad excentricum}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{54.90mm}\smash{\raisebox{-.55\baselineskip}{\rlap{$\left\{\rule[-8.5pt]{0pt}{17pt}\right.$}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{58.30mm}\smash{\rlap{Almag.}}}\rlap{\hspace*{77.90mm}\smash{\llap{4}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{83.00mm}\smash{\llap{30}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{92.90mm}\smash{\llap{2}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{98.30mm}\smash{\llap{30}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{108.20mm}\smash{\llap{2}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{113.70mm}\smash{\llap{15}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{124.00mm}\smash{\llap{2}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{129.20mm}\smash{\llap{30}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{139.60mm}\smash{\llap{6}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{144.70mm}\smash{\llap{15}\rlap{′}}}}
\par\vspace{0.6mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L026}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{58.30mm}\smash{\rlap{Hypoth.}}}\rlap{\hspace*{77.90mm}\smash{\llap{2}}}\rlap{\hspace*{83.00mm}\smash{\llap{30}}}\rlap{\hspace*{92.90mm}\smash{\llap{1}}}\rlap{\hspace*{98.30mm}\smash{\llap{30}}}\rlap{\hspace*{108.20mm}\smash{\llap{1}}}\rlap{\hspace*{113.70mm}\smash{\llap{50}}}\rlap{\hspace*{124.00mm}\smash{\llap{3}}}\rlap{\hspace*{129.20mm}\smash{\llap{30}}}\rlap{\hspace*{139.60mm}\smash{\llap{6}}}\rlap{\hspace*{144.70mm}\smash{\llap{30}}}}
\par\vspace{1.2mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L027}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}\smash{\raisebox{-.55\baselineskip}{\rlap{Inclinatio excentrici ad eclipticam}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{54.90mm}\smash{\raisebox{-.55\baselineskip}{\rlap{$\left\{\rule[-9.5pt]{0pt}{19pt}\right.$}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{58.30mm}\smash{\rlap{Almag.}}}\rlap{\hspace*{77.90mm}\smash{\llap{2}}}\rlap{\hspace*{83.00mm}\smash{\llap{30}}}\rlap{\hspace*{92.90mm}\smash{\llap{1}}}\rlap{\hspace*{98.30mm}\smash{\llap{30}}}\rlap{\hspace*{108.20mm}\smash{\llap{1}}}\rlap{\hspace*{113.70mm}\smash{\llap{0}}}\rlap{\hspace*{124.00mm}\smash{\llap{0}}}\rlap{\hspace*{129.20mm}\smash{\llap{10}}}\rlap{\hspace*{139.60mm}\smash{\llap{0}}}\rlap{\hspace*{144.70mm}\smash{\llap{45}}}}
\par\vspace{1.6mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L028}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{58.30mm}\smash{\rlap{Hypoth.}}}\rlap{\hspace*{77.90mm}\smash{\llap{2}}}\rlap{\hspace*{83.00mm}\smash{\llap{30}}}\rlap{\hspace*{92.90mm}\smash{\llap{1}}}\rlap{\hspace*{98.30mm}\smash{\llap{30}}}\rlap{\hspace*{108.20mm}\smash{\llap{1}}}\rlap{\hspace*{113.70mm}\smash{\llap{50}}}\rlap{\hspace*{124.00mm}\smash{\llap{0}}}\rlap{\hspace*{129.20mm}\smash{\llap{10}}}\rlap{\hspace*{139.60mm}\smash{\llap{0}}}\rlap{\hspace*{144.70mm}\smash{\llap{10}}}}
\par\vspace{1mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0704-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L029}{}{\Indent In \textit{Inscriptione Canopi} quantitates Almagesti occurrunt, praeter inclinationes excentri-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L030}{30}{corum Saturni (2°26′) et Iovis (1°24′).}
\par\vspace{8mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0704-H31}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L031}{}{\CenterLine{\textit{Ad pag. 142–143.} — \textbf{Apparitiones et occultationes planetarum.}}}
\par\vspace{5mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0704-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L032}{}{\Indent Tabulae memoratae t. I, p. 117–18, cap. XLVIII, quo et ipsarum usus docetur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0704-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L033}{}{\Indent De his apparitionibus et occultationibus respectu Solis exorientibus, \textgreek{φάσεις καὶ κρύψεις πρὸς}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L034}{}{\textgreek{τὸν ἥλιον γινόμεναι}, agit \Name{Ptolemaeus} XIII, 7–10 (ed. Halma, t. II, p. 416–31), qui tabulas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L035}{35}{ab Albatenianis necessario diversas praebet XIII, 10 (Halma, II, 430–31).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0704-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L036}{}{\Indent Pauca praemittenda sunt, quae \Name{Ptolemaeus} et \Name{al-Battānī} exponere negligunt; a}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L037}{}{\Name{Barhebraeo}, \textit{Astron.} p. 78–79 text., 66–68 vers., luculenter enarrata. \textit{Superiores pla-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-body-L038}{}{\textit{netae,} quia Sole tardiores, veluti stellae fixae apparitionem semper habent matutinam, occul-}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-notes_left-L001}{}{minime ad latitudines planetarum explicandas suf-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-notes_left-L002}{}{ficere (p. 192), et Almagesti de hac re doctrinam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-notes_left-L003}{}{breviter exponit, omnibus omissis quae ad mathe-}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-notes_right-L001}{}{maticam supputationem inserviunt (p. 192-198). —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-notes_right-L002}{}{P. 191 numeri excentricitatum deferentis Mercurii}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0704-notes_right-L003}{}{et Veneris erroribus affecti sunt.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0705}{256}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L001}{}{tationem semper vespertinam; orientales, seu matutini, seu Solem praecedentes, evadunt a}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L002}{}{coniunctione cum Sole (quae fit superioribus planetis apogeum epicycli occupantibus) ad oppo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L003}{}{sitionem (in epicycli perigeo), hoc est in sinistro epicycli semicirculo; occidentales, seu ve-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L004}{}{spertini, seu Solem subsequentes, ab oppositione ad coniunctionem, hoc est in dextero epicycli}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L005}{5}{semicirculo. — \textit{Inferiores planetae,} Sole velociores, duplicem habent apparitionem et duplicem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L006}{}{occultationem, matutinam scilicet et vespertinam. Orientales evadunt a coniunctione cum Sole}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L007}{}{in perigeo epicycli ad alteram coniunctionem in apogeo, hoc est in dextero epicycli semicirculo;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L008}{}{occidentales a coniunctione apogea ad perigeam, idest in epicycli semicirculo sinistro. Post}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L009}{}{coniunctionem enim in epicycli perigeo, quo retrogradi sunt, Solem in ortu antecedunt; qua}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L010}{10}{re mane in ortu apparent, usque quo Sol ab iis quantitate semidiametri epicycli (1) elongatus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L011}{}{sit. Tunc incipiunt Soli appropinquare, et mane in oriente occultantur. Iterum coniunguntur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L012}{}{Soli cum apogeum epicycli attingunt; directo semper cursu ab apogeo et Sole discedentes,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L013}{}{vespere in occidente cernuntur, usque quo a Sole quantitate semidiametri epicycli elongati sint.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L014}{}{Soli tunc appropinquant, et vespere in occidente occultantur usque ad novam coniunctionem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L015}{15}{perigeam.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0705-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L016}{}{\Indent Tabulae pro data terrestri latitudine exhibent planetarum elongationes apparentes a Sole}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L017}{}{vero, tempore apparitionis et occultationis (\textgreek{αἱ φαινόμεναι τῶν ἀνατολῶν καὶ κρύψεων ἀπὸ τοῦ}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L018}{}{\textgreek{ἀκριβοῦς ἡλίου διαστάσεις}), cum planetae in initio singulorum signorum zodiaci ponantur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0705-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L019}{}{\Indent Elongationes, quae ad apparitiones et occultationes requiruntur, tribus causis variant: di-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L020}{20}{versis planetarum magnitudinibus, diversis inclinationibus horizontis ad eclipticam, variatione}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L021}{}{latitudinum planetarum. \Name{Ptolemaeus} arcum delegit qui variationem nonnisi ob diversas pla-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L022}{}{netarum magnitudines patiretur; estque arcus circuli maximi per horizontis polos transeuntis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L023}{}{(scilicet circuli verticalis), interceptus inter horizontem et Solis centrum eo tempore quo pla-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L024}{}{neta in horizonte apparet aut occultatur. Hic arcus ab Arabibus nostratibusque \textit{arcus visionis}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L025}{25}{appellatur. — Compluribus observationibus, Chaldaeorum potissimum, fretus, statuit XIII, 7}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L026}{}{(ed. Halma, t. II, p. 419) generaliter (\textgreek{ὡς ἐπίπαν}) in initio Cancri (2) Saturnum apparere 14° zo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L027}{}{diaci elongatum a Sole vero, Iovem 12° $\qfrac{3}{4}$, Martem 14° $\qfrac{1}{2}$, Venerem vespere 5° $\qfrac{2}{3}$, Mer-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L028}{}{curium vespere 11° $\qfrac{2}{8}$. Factae sunt hae observationes in climate Phoeniciae, cuius longissimus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L029}{}{dies 14\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} $\qfrac{1}{4}$ horas aequinoctiales complectitur. Sit nunc BA horizon, DEG ecliptica, ZBD arcus}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.316\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0705-body-F01}{}\hspace*{1.5mm}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0705-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.664\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.336\FullSourceWidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L030}{30}{circuli altitudinis seu verticalis qui per centrum D Solis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L031}{}{transit; item E punctum quo planeta latitudine carens,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L032}{}{H aut T punctum quo planeta latitudine praeditus oritur.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L033}{}{Ducantur HK, TL perpendiculares (3). Ob parvitatem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L034}{}{horum arcuum omnium, pro iis possunt ipsorum \textit{chordae}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L035}{35}{sumi, a quibus sensibiliter (\textgreek{πρὸς αἴσϑησιν}) non differunt.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L036}{}{De planetarum latitudinibus agens, demonstraverat \Name{Pto-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L037}{}{\Name{lemaeus} Saturnum ac Iovem fere in ecliptica esse cum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L038}{}{in initio Cancri oriantur, Martem vero latitudinem bo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L039}{}{realem fere 0°12′ sortiri. Ergo sit E situs Saturni et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-body-L040}{40}{Iovis orientium, H situs Martis orientis (eiusque latitudo}
\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0705-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) Id est Veneri 43\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} $\qfrac{1}{2}$ partium qualium semi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-notes_left-L002}{}{diametrus deferentis est 60; Mercurio 22\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} $\qfrac{1}{2}$. Fra-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-notes_left-L003}{}{ctione neglecta, hinc intelliguntur numeri anomaliae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-notes_left-L004}{}{verae quos \Name{al-Battānī} t. I, p. 117, l. 12-19 adducit;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-notes_left-L005}{}{Veneri scilicet 180° ± 43°, Mercurio 90° + 22° et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-notes_left-L006}{}{270° — 22°.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0705-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-notes_right-L001}{}{\Indent (2) Expediebat has observationes facere, Sole}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-notes_right-L002}{}{prope solstitium aestivum existente, quia tunc aer}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-notes_right-L003}{}{quam maxime tenuis est ac purus in nostris cli-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-notes_right-L004}{}{matibus.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0705-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-notes_right-L005}{}{\Indent (3) Ad eclipticam, non ad horizontem sicut in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0705-notes_right-L006}{}{figuris ed. Halma.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0706}{257}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L001}{}{borealis HK proxime); quantitas lineae vel arcus visionis BD pro quoque planeta invariabilis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L002}{}{ut diximus; arcus DE eclipticae erit elongatio (observationibus antea memoratis comperta) Sa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L003}{}{turni aut Iovis a Sole vero D, arcus DK elongatio nota Martis. Angulus BED horizontis et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L004}{}{eclipticae (1) cognoscitur praeceptis II, 11 (ed. Halma, t. I, p. 120–22), quae pro puncto E ecli-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L005}{5}{pticae ita trigonometrice redduntur:}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L006}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{25.90mm}sin ang. $=R\,\dfrac{\text{sin altit.\ gradus culminantis}}{\text{sin arcus eclipticae inter }E\text{ et gradum culmin.}}$\,.}}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L007}{}{Pro Phoeniciae climate et ad initium E Cancri, hunc angulum BED supputat « esse 103 talium}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L008}{}{« [partium], qualium \textit{duo} anguli recti 360 sunt, rationem igitur rectarum circa angulos rectos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L009}{}{« [consistentium] ut 94 ad 75 proxime, et hypotenusas 120° earundem [partium]; » (2) quibus}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L010}{10}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}verbis vult}\rlap{\hspace*{20.20mm}$BED=\dfrac{103^\circ}{2}=51^\circ$ $\qfrac{1}{2}$,\quad unde, triangulis figurae pro rectilineis habitis,}}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L011}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{32.60mm}$HK:KE=94:75$\,,}\rlap{\hspace*{81.30mm}$DE:DB=120:94$\,,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L012}{}{sive, ut nostra utar trigonometria,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L013}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{24.40mm}$\dfrac{KE}{HK}=\dfrac{\text{cotg}\,BED}{R}=\dfrac{75}{94}$\,,}\rlap{\hspace*{83.60mm}$\dfrac{DB}{DE}=\dfrac{\sin BED}{R}=\dfrac{94}{120}$\,.}}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L014}{}{Tunc manifeste pro Saturno et Iove in initio E Cancri collocatis:}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L015}{15}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{47.20mm}arcus visionis $=BD=DE\,\dfrac{94}{120}$\,,}}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L016}{}{ubi DE notus (14° et 12° 45′) ex observationibus antea memoratis. Pro Marte in H consistenti}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L017}{}{eadem inservit formula; sed tunc DK, non DE, ex observationibus illis cognoscitur, qua re}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L018}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{20.90mm}$DE=DK+KE=DK+HK\,\dfrac{75}{94}=14^\circ\,30'+0^\circ\,12'\,\dfrac{75}{94}=14^\circ\,40'$\,.}}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L019}{}{Similiter et in inferiores planetas operans, \textit{arcum visionis} BD invenit 11° Saturno, 10° Iovi,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L020}{20}{11° $\qfrac{1}{2}$ Marti, 5° Veneri, 10° Mercurio; quae quantitates invariabiles sunt.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0706-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L021}{}{\Indent Elongationes a Sole vero in apparitionibus et occultationibus facile nunc est supputare,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L022}{}{quolibet eclipticae puncto in E constituto. \textit{Angulus} BED elicitur formula antea memorata;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L023}{}{\textit{latitudo} HK vel TL planetae nota; arcus DE, cum \Name{Ptolemaeo} triangulis illis tanquam}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L024}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}proxime rectilineis habitis, erit}\rlap{\hspace*{56.70mm}$DE=R\,\dfrac{BD}{\sin BED}$\,.}\rlap{\hspace*{93.40mm}Postea}}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L025}{25}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{29.00mm}$KE=\dfrac{HK\,\text{cotg}\,BED}{R}$\,,}\rlap{\hspace*{69.10mm}vel}\rlap{\hspace*{81.20mm}$EL=\dfrac{TL\,\text{cotg}\,BED}{R}$\,,}}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L026}{}{unde quantitates quaesitae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L027}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{34.80mm}$DK=DE-KE$\,,}\rlap{\hspace*{72.00mm}vel}\rlap{\hspace*{84.00mm}$DL=DE+EL$\,.}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0706-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L028}{}{\Indent Verae regulae, quales e consideratione triangulorum sphaericorum eliciuntur, erant quidem:}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L029}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{24.30mm}sin $DE=R\,\dfrac{\sin BD}{\sin BED}$\,,}\rlap{\hspace*{69.50mm}sin $KE=\dfrac{\text{tang}\,HK\,\text{cotg}\,BED}{R}$\,,}}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L030}{30}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{48.00mm}sin $EL=\dfrac{\text{tang}\,TL\,\text{cotg}\,BED}{R}$\,;}}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-body-L031}{}{sed in parvis his triangulis differentia prorsus negligenda.}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0706-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) Est angulus contentus horizonte et portione}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-notes_left-L002}{}{eclipticae supra horizontem exstante; aequalis in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-notes_left-L003}{}{puncto eclipticae orienti et in puncto eclipticae dia-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-notes_left-L004}{}{metraliter opposito tunc occidenti. Haud opportune}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-notes_left-L005}{}{igitur saepe \textit{angulus orientis} aut \textit{orientalis} vocatur.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0706-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-notes_right-L001}{}{\Indent (2) \textgreek{Τὴν ὑπὸ ΒΕΔ γωνίαν εὑρίσκομεν τοιούτων ργ},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-notes_right-L002}{}{\textgreek{οἵων αἱ δύο ὀρθαὶ τξ, καὶ τὸν λόγον διὰ τούτου τῶν}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-notes_right-L003}{}{\textgreek{περὶ τὰς ὀρθὰς γωνίας τὸν τῶν ϟδ πρὸς τὰ οε ἔγγιστα},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-notes_right-L004}{}{\textgreek{τοιούτων δὲ καὶ τὰς ὑποτεινούσας ρκ} (ed. Halma, t. II,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0706-notes_right-L005}{}{p. 419).}
\end{minipage}
\par\vspace{5mm}\hfill{\fontsize{8}{10}\selectfont 33}\hspace{10mm}
\DSource{0707}{258}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0707-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L001}{}{\Indent Ad nostram igitur scribendi rationem aptata, haec est regula \textit{Ptolemaica:}}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L002}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{22.50mm}elongatio a \SunSym{} $=R\,\dfrac{\text{arcus visionis}}{\sin BED}\mp\dfrac{\text{latit.\ planetae}\times\text{cotg}\,BED}{R}$\,;}}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L003}{}{ubi angulus BED intelligitur ad planetae situm effectus, si planeta latitudine caruerit; ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L004}{}{punctum eclipticae simul cum planeta ascendens, si planeta latitudinem habuerit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0707-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L005}{5}{\Indent Hac ratione \Name{Ptolemaeus} XIII, 9 (ed. Halma, t. II, p. 427–28) dicit tabulas construxisse,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L006}{}{quibus, longitudine [vera] planetarum in initiis signorum constituta, elongationes apparentes}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L007}{}{planetarum a Sole vero exhibentur necessariae ad apparitiones et occultationes (\textgreek{τὰς φαινομένας}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L008}{}{\textgreek{τῶν ἀνατολῶν καὶ κρύψεων ἀπὸ τοῦ ἀκριβοῦς ἡλίου διαστάσεις ὡς αὐτῶν τῶν ἀστέρων ἐν ταῖς ἀρχαῖς}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L009}{}{\textgreek{τῶν δωδεκατημορίων ὑποκειμένων, ὑπετάξαμεν}, l. c., Halma, II, 428), seu arcus eclipticae inter ini-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L010}{10}{tium signi a planeta tunc apparenti aut occidenti occupatum et Solem verum. Si planeta extra}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L011}{}{signorum initia fuerit, tabulae interpolandae erunt, sicut etiam \Name{al-Battānī} t. I, p. 118,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L012}{}{l. 18–25, docet. — Tabula \textit{Ptolemaica} (1) est ad Phoeniciae clima, seu ad latitudinem circiter}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L013}{}{33° condita; ab \textit{Albateniana} his causis discrepare debet: 1°) ob diversam eclipticae obliqui-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L014}{}{tatem, unde anguli BED diversi fiunt; — 2°) ob diversam latitudinem terrestrem ad quam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L015}{15}{tabulae referuntur; — 3°) ob longum temporis intervallum, quo longitudines nodorum plane-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L016}{}{tarum auctae sunt motu praecessionis, ita ut dato eclipticae loco latitudo planetae mutata sit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0707-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L017}{}{\Indent Quanam ratione latitudines planetarum in tabulis his conficiendis supputaverint, nec \Name{Pto-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L018}{}{\Name{lemaeus} nec \Name{al-Battānī} docent; frustra ego permultum temporis et laboris in tabularum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L019}{}{constructionem enucleandam impendi. Nec rem attentaverunt (aut fortasse attentatam dereli-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L020}{20}{querunt) \Name{Purbachius}, \Name{Regiomontanus}, \Name{Maginus}, \Name{Riccioli} aliique ab XV ad XVII}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L021}{}{saec. astronomi, et \Name{Delambre} atque recentiores qui historiam astronomiae narraverunt. Im-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L022}{}{possibile igitur fuit numeros codicis certissime emendare.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0707-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L023}{}{\Indent Quae frustra expertus sum longius ac inutile hic est referre; nonnullas considerationes}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L024}{}{tamen addere iuvat.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0707-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L025}{25}{\Indent \textit{Species apparitionis} aut \textit{occultationis} colligitur ex anomalia aequata, i. e. ex arcu epicycli}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L026}{}{quem planeta ab apogeo vero epicycli peregit. Superiores enim planetae paulo post apogeum epi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L027}{}{cycli prope sunt apparitionem matutinam, paulo ante prope occultationem vespertinam. Planetae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L028}{}{inferiores paulo post apogeum epicycli appropinquant apparitioni vespertinae, paulo ante apogeum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L029}{}{occultationi matutinae; paulo ante perigeum vespertinae occultationi, paulo post perigeum epicycli}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L030}{30}{matutinae apparitioni. Quantitates anomaliae proxime necessarias ad apparitiones et occultationes}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-body-L031}{}{\Name{Theon} ac \Name{al-Battānī} (t. I, p. 117) paulo aliter quam \Name{Ḥabash} (f. 67,v.—68,r.) constituunt:}
\par\vspace{4.5mm}
