\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0707-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0707-notes-L001}{}{\Indent (1) In edit. \Name{Halma} aliquot mendis scatet; consulenda igitur editio a \Name{Heiberg} curata.}
}
\DSource{0708}{259}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0708-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L001}{}{\Indent Nescio quo modo hi numeri supputati (\textgreek{παχυμέστερον} « rudissime » ut \Name{Theon} I, 63 dicit);}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L002}{}{discrepantiae nullius momenti prorsus in calculo latitudinum.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0708-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L003}{}{\Indent Anguli BED, eclipticae et horizontis, aequales fiunt ad eclipticae puncta pariter ab uno}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L004}{}{aequinoctiorum dissita; item angulus ad punctum eclipticae oriens aequatur angulo ad oppo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L005}{5}{situm punctum quod simul occidit. Ergo, si planetae semper in plano eclipticae iacerent, sci-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L006}{}{licet latitudine destituti essent,}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0708-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L007}{}{\Indent\Indent 1° apparitionum (aut occultationum) arcus essent aequales in punctis pariter ab uno}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L008}{}{aequinoctiorum elongatis;}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0708-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L009}{}{\Indent\Indent 2° apparitionis arcus puncti \textit{x} aequarentur arcubus occultationis puncti \textit{x} + 180°;}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0708-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L010}{10}{\Indent\Indent 3° in punctis solstitialibus (90° et 270°), quae pariter ab aequinoctiis distant et diame-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L011}{}{traliter inter se opposita sunt, arcus apparitionum aequarentur arcubus occultationum;}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0708-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L012}{}{\Indent\Indent 4° arcus apparitionum aut occultationum planetarum omnium pro dato eclipticae puncto}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L013}{}{essent proportionales ad arcus visionis ipsorum planetarum; nam, in formula pro planetis in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L014}{}{ecliptica iacentibus, ad idem eclipticae punctum solus arcus visionis variaret.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0708-P07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L015}{15}{\Indent Nunc planetarum superiorum tabulas inspiciamus.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0708-P08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L016}{}{\Indent In \textit{Saturno} apud \Name{Ptolemaeum} aequales sunt numeri apparitionum (aut occultationum)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L017}{}{respondentes signis pariter ab alterutro aequinoctio remotis; discrepantes numeri pro signis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L018}{}{diametraliter oppositis (solstitiis exceptis); discrepantes etiam apparitiones ab occultationibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L019}{}{punctorum oppositorum; demum maximae minimaeque quantitates in aequinoctiis occurrunt.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L020}{20}{Inde patet Saturno esse latitudines \textit{aequales} proxime in longitudinibus a 0° aut 180° aeque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L021}{}{distantibus, \textit{maximas} circa aequinoctia, prope solstitia \textit{nullas;} hoc est Saturni nodos prope}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L022}{}{solstitia (90° et 270°) incidere, sicut etiam e doctrina latitudinum Ptolemaica elucet (1). — Sed}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L023}{}{in tabula \Name{al-Battānī} arcus apparitionis haud multum differt ab arcu occultationis in initiis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L024}{}{Cancri et Capricorni, nec multum (infra 1° semper) discrepant inter se arcus apparitionum (aut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L025}{25}{occultationum) locis ab uno aequinoctiorum aeque distantibus; unde colligitur latitudines Sa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L026}{}{turni \textit{nullas} esse punctis nonnihilo a solstitiis remotis, \textit{maximas} locis nonnihilo ab aequi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L027}{}{noctiis dissitis. Re vera Albatenio longitudo apogei Saturni pro 1191 Dhū ’l-qarnayn est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L028}{}{244° 28′; ergo}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L029}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{27.90mm}longitudo limitis borealis = 244° 28′ — 50° 0′ = 194° 28′}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L030}{30}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{27.80mm}longitudo nodi ascendentis = 194° 28′ — 90° 0′ = 104° 28′.}}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L031}{}{Nodi scilicet in media puncta Cancri et Capricorni cadunt.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0708-P09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L032}{}{\Indent In \textit{Iove} apud \Name{Ptolemaeum} omnia quae in Saturno occurrunt; \textit{nullae} latitudines igitur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L033}{}{paulo a solstitialibus punctis longe, \textit{maximae} circa aequinoctia, \textit{aequales} locis ab alterutro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L034}{}{aequinoctio pariter remotis. Re vera apud Ptolemaeum long. limitis borealis Iovis = long.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L035}{35}{apogei + 20° = 152° 9′ + 20° = 172° 9′; cadunt igitur nodi 8° ante initium Cancri et Capri-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L036}{}{corni. — Nec aliter apud \Name{al-Battānī}, iuxta quem:}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L037}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{32.90mm}limes borealis Iovis = 164° 28′ + 20° 0′ = 184° 28′,}}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L038}{}{ita ut nodi 4° $\qfrac{1}{2}$ post initia Cancri et Capricorni incidant.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0708-P10}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L039}{}{\Indent In \textit{Marte,} apud \Name{Ptolemaeum}, quae de Saturno et Iove diximus non occurrunt; sed}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-body-L040}{40}{arcus apparitionum haud multum differunt ab arcubus occultationum punctorum diametraliter}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0708-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) Longitudo limitis borealis Saturni aequatur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-notes_left-L002}{}{longitudini apogei detractis 50°, ut p. 239, l. 8-9, dixi;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-notes_left-L003}{}{ergo, iuxta Ptolemaeum, 224° 10′ — 50° 0′ = 174° 10′.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-notes_right-L001}{}{Qua re nodus ascendens 174° 10′ — 90° = 84° 10′, seu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-notes_right-L002}{}{haud longe ab initio Cancri; descendens 264° 10′,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0708-notes_right-L003}{}{sex fere gradibus ante initium Capricorni.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0709}{260}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L001}{}{oppositorum. Unde liquet nodos quidem esse a punctis solstitialibus remotos (1), sed latitudines}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L002}{}{planetae temporibus apparitionum et occultationum non esse magnas. — Apud \Name{al-Battānī},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L003}{}{si numeris impressis parvae emendationes inferantur, haec colliguntur:}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0709-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L004}{}{\Indent\Indent 1.° arcum apparitionis initii dati signi aequari arcui occultationis signi de diametro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L005}{5}{oppositi;}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0709-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L006}{}{\Indent\Indent 2.° aequales esse arcus apparitionum (aut occultationum) pro punctis pariter ab alte-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L007}{}{rutro aequinoctio remotis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0709-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L008}{}{\Indent Iuxta al-Battānī, anno 1191 Dhū ’l-qarnayn, long. limitis borealis Martis = long. apogei =}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L009}{}{= 126° 58′; nodi igitur 36° 58′ et 216° 58′, seu longe a punctis aequinoctialibus et solstitialibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L010}{10}{dissitis. Maximae autem latitudines Martis in duabus oppositis plagis Caeli contingunt, nec eadem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L011}{}{quantitate; nam latitudo borealis maxima est 4° 21′, australis 7° 7′. Ergo proprietates tabularum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L012}{}{Martis nuper memoratae nonnisi ex hoc possunt ortae censeri, quod al-Battānī latitudinem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L013}{}{Martis \textit{neglexerit.} Quod mirum non esse e sequentibus patebit: Ad Theonis exemplum, putat}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L014}{}{al-Battānī apparitiones (aut occultationes) Martis tunc esse exspectandas cum anomalia aequata}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L015}{15}{circiter 20° (aut 340°) fuerit. Huic anomaliae respondent in tabulis t. II, p. 140, latitudines}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L016}{}{Martis 0° 11′ in boream, 0° 5′ in austrum; harum latitudinum sexagesimis sumptis iuxta Alba-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L017}{}{tenii praecepta (t. I, p. 115–116), quantitates iis minores eliciuntur, sicut ad initium Arietis — 0° 3′,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L018}{}{Tauri — 0° 0′ 37″, Geminorum + 0° 4′ 17″, Cancri + 0° 8′ 45″, Librae + 0° 6′ 36″, Capri-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L019}{}{corni — 0° 3′ 58″, Aquarii — 0° 4′ 47″, Piscium — 0° 4′ 35″ etc. Tabulae apparitionum ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L020}{20}{\textgreek{φ} = 36° 0′ conditae, ubi maximus angulus BED est 77° 35′ (in Librae initio), minimus 30° 25′}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L021}{}{(in initio Arietis). Cum igitur perpaulae sint latitudines Martis pro instituto nostro sumendae,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L022}{}{magni autem anguli eclipticae ac horizontis, differentia longitudinum planetae et puncti ecli-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L023}{}{pticae cum planeta ascendentis adeo fiet parva ut, absque sensibili errore, possint anguli BED}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L024}{}{(quorum sinu et cotangente uti debemus) ad longitudinem planetae ipsam supputari. Praeterea}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L025}{25}{terminus ∓ lat. plan. × cotg BED Marti plerumque insensibilis fit; maxima enim cotangens}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L026}{}{adhibenda apud al-Battānī est, radio 1 partis sumpto, 1.73. Maximae quantitates illius ter-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L027}{}{mini sunt}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L028}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{30.50mm}\smash{\rlap{+ (0° 3′ × 1.73)}}}\rlap{\hspace*{65.50mm}\smash{\rlap{=}}}\rlap{\hspace*{69.90mm}\smash{\rlap{+}}}\rlap{\hspace*{74.30mm}\smash{\rlap{0° 5′.19}}}\rlap{\hspace*{93.70mm}\smash{\rlap{in initio Arietis,}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L029}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{30.50mm}\smash{\rlap{— (0° 8′.75 × 0.836)}}}\rlap{\hspace*{65.50mm}\smash{\rlap{=}}}\rlap{\hspace*{69.90mm}\smash{\rlap{—}}}\rlap{\hspace*{74.30mm}\smash{\rlap{0° 7′.31}}}\rlap{\hspace*{93.70mm}\smash{\rlap{in initio Cancri;}}}}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L030}{30}{longe minores et etiam fere \textit{nullae} in aliis signis. — Poterat igitur al-Battānī tam parvas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L031}{}{emendationes, ex latitudine exorientes, negligere in supputandis tabulis nonnisi ad rudiores}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L032}{}{calculos inservientibus.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0709-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L033}{}{\Indent Neglecta latitudine, nobis etiam facilis fieri debet tabularum constructio; tamen, calculis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L034}{}{meis ductis, quantitates invenio multum ab Albatenianis discrepantes. Tria exempla adducam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L035}{35}{numerorum qui, ut ex collatione cum variis lectionibus infra exhibenda apparebit, in codice}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L036}{}{Escurialensi certissimi sunt; arcus nempe apparitionum in initiis Arietis, Leonis, Virginis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L037}{}{quibus arcus occultationum in Libra, Aquario, Piscibus aequantur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0709-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L038}{}{\Indent Primo anguli BED, ad initia E signorum ascendentia et latitudinem terrestrem \textgreek{φ} = 36°,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L039}{}{supputandi:}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0709-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L040}{40}{\Indent gradus culminans = arcus eclipticae respondens quantitati (ascens. obl. E + 90°), tamquam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-body-L041}{}{ascensio recta consideratae (2);}
\par\vspace{6pt}\hrule height.25pt\vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0709-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-notes_left-L001}{}{\Indent (1) Limes borealis Martis longitudinem habet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-notes_left-L002}{}{apogei; Ptolemaeo 106° 40′. Nodi igitur 16° 40′ et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-notes_left-L003}{}{196° 40′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0709-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-notes_left-L004}{}{\Indent (2) In tabulis Albatenii, ascensiones rectas}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-notes_right-L001}{}{auctas 90° praebentibus, sufficit ascensionem obli-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-notes_right-L002}{}{quam E, absque augmento, in tabula ascensionum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-notes_right-L003}{}{rectarum quaerere, arcumque eclipticae correspon-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0709-notes_right-L004}{}{dentem sumere.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0710}{261}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L001}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.40mm}\smash{\rlap{altitudo gradus culminantis = (90° — \textgreek{φ}) ± \textgreek{δ} = 54° ± \textgreek{δ}}}}}
\par\vspace{1mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L002}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.50mm}sin $BED=R\,\dfrac{\text{sin altit.\ gradus culm.}}{\text{sin arcus eclipt.\ inter }E\text{ et gradum culm.}}$\,.}}
\par\vspace{1mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L003}{}{Ergo pro 0° longitudinis (initio Arietis):}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L004}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.10mm}\smash{\rlap{asc. obl. 0° = 0°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{61.40mm}\smash{\rlap{grad. culm. = 270°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{111.30mm}\smash{\rlap{\textgreek{δ} =}}}\rlap{\hspace*{122.90mm}\smash{\llap{23}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{128.70mm}\smash{\llap{35}\rlap{′}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L005}{5}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.40mm}\smash{\rlap{alt. gr. culm. = 54° — 23° 35′ = 30° 25′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{79.60mm}\smash{\rlap{sin}}}\rlap{\hspace*{91.30mm}\smash{\llap{30}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{97.10mm}\smash{\llap{25}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{117.00mm}\smash{\rlap{9.704 3947}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L006}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{59.00mm}\smash{\rlap{$BED$ = 30° 25′}}}}
\par\vspace{2.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L007}{}{Pro 120° (initio Leonis):}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L008}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.10mm}\smash{\rlap{asc. obl. 120° = 106° 37′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{61.30mm}\smash{\rlap{grad. culm. = 18° 2′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{111.30mm}\smash{\rlap{\textgreek{δ} =}}}\rlap{\hspace*{122.90mm}\smash{\llap{7}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{128.70mm}\smash{\llap{7}\rlap{′}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L009}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.10mm}\smash{\rlap{alt. gr. culm. = 54° + 7° 7′ = 61° 7′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{79.40mm}\smash{\rlap{sin}}}\rlap{\hspace*{91.30mm}\smash{\llap{61}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{97.10mm}\smash{\llap{7}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{117.00mm}\smash{\rlap{9.942 3083}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L010}{10}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{42.60mm}\smash{\rlap{sin ($E$ — gr. culm.) = sin}}}\rlap{\hspace*{91.30mm}\smash{\llap{101}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{97.10mm}\smash{\llap{58}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{117.00mm}\smash{\rlap{9.990 4580}}}}
\par\nointerlineskip\vspace{1pt}\noindent\rlap{\hspace*{116.90mm}\rule{16.50mm}{.4pt}}\par\nointerlineskip\vspace{2pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L011}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{51.80mm}\smash{\rlap{$BED$ = 63° 30′ 57″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{117.00mm}\smash{\rlap{9.951 8503}}}}
\par\vspace{2.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L012}{}{Pro 150° (initio Virginis):}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L013}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.10mm}\smash{\rlap{asc. obl. 150° = 143° 35′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{61.30mm}\smash{\rlap{grad. culm. = 55° 36′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{111.30mm}\smash{\rlap{\textgreek{δ} =}}}\rlap{\hspace*{122.90mm}\smash{\llap{19}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{128.70mm}\smash{\llap{21}\rlap{′}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L014}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.10mm}\smash{\rlap{alt. gr. culm. = 54° + 19° 21′ = 73° 21′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{79.50mm}\smash{\rlap{sin}}}\rlap{\hspace*{91.30mm}\smash{\llap{73}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{97.10mm}\smash{\llap{21}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{117.00mm}\smash{\rlap{9.981 3986}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L015}{15}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{42.90mm}\smash{\rlap{sin ($E$ — gr. culm.) = sin}}}\rlap{\hspace*{91.30mm}\smash{\llap{94}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{97.10mm}\smash{\llap{4}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{117.00mm}\smash{\rlap{9.998 9052}}}}
\par\nointerlineskip\vspace{1pt}\noindent\rlap{\hspace*{116.90mm}\rule{16.50mm}{.4pt}}\par\nointerlineskip\vspace{2pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L016}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{52.10mm}\smash{\rlap{$BED$ = 73° 50′ 26″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{117.00mm}\smash{\rlap{9.982 4934}}}}
\par\vspace{2mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L017}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.10mm}Supputandi postea}\rlap{\hspace*{43.80mm}arcus appar. $=R\,\dfrac{\text{arcus visionis}}{\sin BED}$\,,}\rlap{\hspace*{101.50mm}ubi arcus visionis est 11° 30′ =}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L018}{}{= 41400″.}
\par\vspace{0.5mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L019}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{51.00mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{0° (Arietis)}}}\rlap{\hspace*{86.60mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{120° (Leonis)}}}\rlap{\hspace*{122.40mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{150° (Virginis)}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L020}{20}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{15.30mm}\smash{\rlap{arc. visionis}}}\rlap{\hspace*{43.80mm}\smash{\rlap{4.617 0003}}}\rlap{\hspace*{79.50mm}\smash{\rlap{4.617 0003}}}\rlap{\hspace*{115.60mm}\smash{\rlap{4.617 0003}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L021}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{15.30mm}\smash{\rlap{sin $BED$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{43.80mm}\smash{\rlap{9.704 3947}}}\rlap{\hspace*{79.50mm}\smash{\rlap{9.951 8503}}}\rlap{\hspace*{115.50mm}\smash{\rlap{9.982 4934}}}}
\par\nointerlineskip\vspace{1pt}\noindent\rlap{\hspace*{43.70mm}\rule{16.50mm}{.4pt}}\rlap{\hspace*{79.40mm}\rule{16.50mm}{.4pt}}\rlap{\hspace*{115.40mm}\rule{16.50mm}{.4pt}}\par\nointerlineskip\vspace{2pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L022}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{43.80mm}\smash{\rlap{4.912 6056}}}\rlap{\hspace*{79.70mm}\smash{\rlap{4.665 1500}}}\rlap{\hspace*{115.50mm}\smash{\rlap{4.634 5069}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L023}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{34.10mm}\smash{\rlap{81772″ = 22° 42′ 52″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{71.40mm}\smash{\rlap{46254″ = 12° 50′ 54″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{108.70mm}\smash{\rlap{43103″ = 11° 58′ 23″}}}}
\par\vspace{2mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L024}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.50mm}\smash{\rlap{Ergo:}}}\rlap{\hspace*{31.60mm}\smash{\rlap{Arieti}}}\rlap{\hspace*{73.80mm}\smash{\rlap{Leoni}}}\rlap{\hspace*{113.40mm}\smash{\rlap{Virgini}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L025}{25}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.30mm}\smash{\rlap{al-Battānī}}}\rlap{\hspace*{35.70mm}\smash{\llap{29}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{41.70mm}\smash{\llap{0}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{76.60mm}\smash{\llap{16}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{82.60mm}\smash{\llap{7}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{117.40mm}\smash{\llap{15}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{123.40mm}\smash{\llap{8}\rlap{′}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L026}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.40mm}\smash{\rlap{mea supputatio}}}\rlap{\hspace*{35.70mm}\smash{\llap{22}}}\rlap{\hspace*{41.70mm}\smash{\llap{43}}}\rlap{\hspace*{76.60mm}\smash{\llap{12}}}\rlap{\hspace*{82.60mm}\smash{\llap{51}}}\rlap{\hspace*{117.40mm}\smash{\llap{11}}}\rlap{\hspace*{123.40mm}\smash{\llap{58}}}}
\par\nointerlineskip\vspace{1pt}\noindent\rlap{\hspace*{31.30mm}\rule{11.50mm}{.4pt}}\rlap{\hspace*{72.40mm}\rule{11.30mm}{.4pt}}\rlap{\hspace*{113.20mm}\rule{11.30mm}{.4pt}}\par\nointerlineskip\vspace{2pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L027}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{8.00mm}\smash{\rlap{differentia}}}\rlap{\hspace*{35.70mm}\smash{\llap{6}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{41.70mm}\smash{\llap{17}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{76.60mm}\smash{\llap{3}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{82.60mm}\smash{\llap{16}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{117.40mm}\smash{\llap{3}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{123.40mm}\smash{\llap{10}\rlap{′}}}}
\par\vspace{2.5mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0710-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L028}{}{\Indent Tantae sunt hae discrepantiae ut nullo modo eas explicare possim. Alias multas expe-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L029}{}{rientias quas institueram hic referre inutile puto.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0710-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L030}{30}{\Indent Utrum \Name{al-Battānī} has tabulas (quibus \textit{ne nimium confidatur monet}) ipse condiderit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L031}{}{an ab aliis Arabicis astronomis iam supputatas acceperit, incertum erat. Cum codex Escuria-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L032}{}{lensis vocalibus destitutus sit, locus t. III, p. 178, l. 4–7: \ArabicRun{وقد وضعت لها اقدار الرؤْية بدرج البروج في كل}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L033}{}{\ArabicRun{اقليم على ما رصد من ظهورها في كل برج. ورسم ذلك في رؤس البروج وان كان ذلك ليس على الحقيقة من قبل ما يعرض من}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L034}{}{\ArabicRun{اختلاف عروضها ولذلك اقتصرنا على اثبات هذه الاقدار في اقليم واحد .....}, duplicem interpretationem sortitur ob}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L035}{35}{duplicem modum quo vox \ArabicRun{وضعت} signis vocalium instrui potest. Nam, si \ArabicRun{وُضِعَتْ} legatur, sensus fit:}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L036}{}{« iis (i. e. planetis) \textit{constitutae sunt} quantitates apparitionis, in signorum gradibus, pro omni}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L037}{}{« climate, iuxta id apparitionum eorum quod observatum est in singulis signis; idque in initiis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L038}{}{« signorum descriptum est, licet non sit ad veritatem ob variationem in eorum (planetarum)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L039}{}{« latitudinibus exorientem. Qua re satis habuimus has quantitates ad unum tantum clima no-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0710-body-L040}{40}{« tare .... ». Si \ArabicRun{وَضَعْتُ} legatur, ita erit vertendum: « iis \textit{constitui} (vel: \textit{constitueram}) quan-}
\end{document}
